- `frontend/src/types.ts` mirrors every post-Phase-4 sheet field (all optional so older stored resolutions still type-check) plus `SheetFeature`, `SheetFeat`.
- The phone export path (download via `/api/export/<fmt>`) is unchanged; `tests/test_app.py::test_all_export_formats_carry_features_through_the_api` posts a draft to resolve + yaml/markdown/pdf.
- Tests: 860 server tests passing.
- Not done: no UI panel lists `sheet.features` (exports only); spell-slot-based features (counts of uses) are not computed.

## Smaller cleanups (Slice 12, 2026-10-09)
- Ability methods are validated server-side (`_ability_method_problems`, blocking `problems`): `pointbuy` needs every score in 8-15 (`point-buy-out-of-range`) and at most 27 points spent (`point-buy-over-budget`; unspent points are fine); `standard` must be a permutation of 15,14,13,12,10,8 (`standard-array-mismatch`; the untouched all-10 default is not flagged); `roll` needs 3-18 per score (`roll-out-of-range`); `manual` is unchecked. The frontend's `roll` method now exists: a "Roll all six scores" button (4d6 drop lowest, `crypto.getRandomValues`) dispatching `SET_ABILITY_SCORES`.
- `preferences.advancementType` (milestone/XP) was never read by anything and is removed from the type, the reducer and the Home page; old stored drafts that still carry it are harmless (the server ignores unknown preference keys).
- `spells.spell_slots_for(catalog, class_entries)` owns the single-class-vs-combined-table rule and the separate Pact Magic pool; `resolve()` calls it.
- `catalog/official_source.py` now *appends* the repo root to `sys.path` (it was prepended, so the v1 `app.py` could shadow `server/app.py` in a test run where a catalog-importing test sorted before `test_app.py`). `tests/test_chargen_isolation.py` knows `werkzeug`, `export_fields` and the sibling test import (it had been failing since Slice 1).
- README rewritten for v2 (setup, run, ports 8000/5173, `HOST=0.0.0.0`, phone testing, tests); this document's backend layout, test count and two stale "known gaps" (secondary-class proficiency card, slot-selection split) were corrected. The secondary-class gap was verified closed with Bard/Ranger/Rogue/Wizard as the second class.
- Tests: 874 server tests passing (`tests/test_ability_methods.py`, new `spell_slots_for` cases).

## Attacks, carrying capacity, armor warnings (Slice 9, 2026-10-09; 9c Defense was Slice 2)
- `engine/attacks.py` (pure): `weapon_attack(item, modifiers, prof_bonus, proficiency_ids, styles)` and `attacks_from_gear(...)` (deduped by item index, first-seen order). `derived.attacks` = `[{itemId, name, ability, proficient, attackBonus, damage, damageType, versatileDamage, offhandDamage, duelingDamage, mastery, range, notes}]` (`duelingDamage` since S5, `mastery` since C2), one per distinct equipped weapon (granted, chosen and equipped `added` items, same notion of "equipped" as AC). Ranged non-finesse -> DEX; finesse -> the higher of STR/DEX (tie STR); melee incl. thrown melee -> STR. Proficiency: `simple-weapons`/`martial-weapons` by category, or `reference_data.weapon_proficiency_id(index)` (index + "s", overrides for the three crossbows) against the weapon proficiencies only. `range` prefers `throw_range` when the item has one (dagger/handaxe "20/60"), else `range` ("150/600", "5"). Net has no damage. Damage is bare dice at modifier 0 ("1d6"), the blowgun's "1" becomes "1+2" etc.
- Fighting styles reuse `CLASS_FEATURE_EFFECTS`: Archery (`attack_bonus.ranged`), Dueling (`damage_bonus.one_handed_melee`, melee and not two-handed, never on versatile damage, note "Dueling: only with no other weapon"), and new `two_weapon_fighting` / `great_weapon_fighting` flags for the fighter/ranger/paladin ids. `features.attack_styles(effects)` collects them for the attack math. Two-Weapon Fighting adds the modifier to off-hand damage (otherwise only a negative one); Great Weapon Fighting is a note on melee two-handed/versatile weapons.
- `reference_data.SPECIFIC_WEAPON_PROFICIENCY_IDS` is the hoisted set that `resolve()` used locally (21 ids), plus `WEAPON_PROFICIENCY_ID_OVERRIDES` / `weapon_proficiency_id`; a test checks every one of the 21 is produced by some SRD weapon.
- `derived.carryingCapacity = {capacity, pushDragLift, carried, overCapacity}` (`derived.carrying_capacity/equipment_weight/carrying_summary`): STR (final score) x 15 x size multiplier (Tiny 0.5, Large 2, every other size x1, as in the plan), carried = sum of qty x weight over `sheet.equipment.granted/chosen/added` (unequipped added items weigh too; background starting gear counts, e.g. Acolyte's 4 lb), rounded to 2 dp. Size is `sheet.species.size` (Slice 8; falls back to the race's `size`, then Medium with no species) and feeds this call in `resolve()`.
- `equipment.armor_warnings(equipped_items, scores, proficiency_ids)` -> non-blocking `warnings` `armor-not-proficient-{index}` (body armor needs its category proficiency or `all-armor`; a shield needs `shields`), `armor-strength-{index}` (str_minimum above STR; message says -10 ft speed, `derived.speed` is NOT changed), `armor-multiple-body` (more than one distinct non-shield armor), and `derived.stealthDisadvantage` (any equipped armor with `stealth_disadvantage`). The proficiency set is `proficient_armor | proficient_weapons`, evaluated after all proficiency sources. Warnings show on the Home page's existing warnings list.
- Exports: `attacks`, `carryingCapacity`, `stealthDisadvantage` are in `export_fields.DERIVED_FIELDS` (YAML) and `extra_blocks` (Markdown/PDF): "Атаки" (one bullet per attack), "Носимост" (`carried/capacity lb`, "(претоварен)" when over) and "Броня" (stealth disadvantage). Frontend: `Attack`, `CarryingCapacity`, optional `Derived` fields in `types.ts`; ExportPage lists attacks and carrying capacity as a plain unstyled list (checked at 375px).
- Out of scope / known limits: Dueling's "no other weapon" hand check (only the note "Dueling: only with no other weapon" unless the draft sets `equipment.wielded`, see G2; Weapon Mastery was added in C2, see the 2024 chassis section), magic items, Monk martial arts for non-Monks, feat attacks, other unarmed strikes (the Monk's own row came with R2) (e.g. Savage Attacker), two copies of a weapon as separate attack rows, the heavy-armor speed penalty, Huge/Gargantuan capacity multipliers, a "crossbows-heavy" id in `SPECIFIC_WEAPON_PROFICIENCY_IDS` (no SRD class/race grants it; `weapon_proficiency_id` maps it anyway). Every added/granted/chosen item counts as equipped, as for AC, unless `equipment.worn` / `wielded` override it (G2). **Superseded (FE5, FE12): feats now add attack rows, off-hand damage and row notes; see the FE overview.**
- Tests: 913 server tests passing (`tests/test_engine_attacks.py`, `tests/test_attacks_resolve.py`, additions to `test_derived.py`, `test_engine_equipment.py`, `test_export_fields.py`).

## Species feats, lineage variants, size (Slice 8, 2026-10-09)
- **8a species feat choice.** `official_source._species_feat_grants_from_5etools` turns a race/subrace `feats` field into `feat_grants` (`[{count, category}]`; `{"any": n}` -> category `None`, `anyFromCategory` -> that category). Exactly three records carry it: Human/XPHB (Origin feat, `O`), Custom Lineage/TCE and Variant Human/PHB (`human-phb-variant-phb`, any). `resolve()` emits one Choice per grant, `species:<race>:feat-<i>` (subrace grants: `species:<race>:subspecies:<sub>:feat-<i>`), source kind `species`, options `feat:<index>` sorted by name. Pool: an explicit category offers that category only; "any" offers feats with category '' (2014 books), `G` and `O` minus "Ability Score Improvement" (EB, D, FS, DG excluded); `draft.sources` filters like every feat. Option states: prerequisite failure (checked against pre-feat scores incl. background picks, at total level, so permissive) wins over "already taken" (a background feat, or an earlier species pick, and the feat is not `repeatable`).
- The picked feat is a shallow catalog copy tagged `_species_origin = "<choice id>:feat:<index>"` (the analogue of `_asi_origin`; `_feat_cid_prefix` and `_feat_instance_key` read both). Its cascaded choices (proficiencies, languages, expertise, saving throws, spell variant/choices, ability-bonus) are ordinary feat-kind Choices with that prefix, stored in `species.choices`. Precedence of duplicates: background -> species -> ASI walk, first pick wins; a stored pick of a disabled option is not applied and reported (`feat-duplicate-<index>-<choice id>` or `feat-prerequisite-unmet-<index>`). The ASI-feat resolution call moved below species resolution to receive the species indices; `held` (feat state for prerequisites) is now built earlier and species feats are added to it. Species-feat ability bonuses (fixed and chosen) join `feat_bonuses` (uncapped when this was written; **superseded by FE2**: clamped to the feat's cap like every origin feat). `sheet.grantedFeats` = background + species + ASI; `sheet.feats[].origin` gains `"species"`.
- Frontend: SpeciesPage shows `species` Choices plus feat Choices whose cid starts `species:`; BackgroundPage and the Background badge exclude `species:` cids; the Species badge counts both.
- Not done / limits: Skilled's cascaded pick was missing here; closed afterwards (see "Feat skill-or-tool picks" below). Prerequisites use total level and pre-feat scores. 2014 category-'' feats are not ASI-eligible (G only), unchanged.
- Tests: 929 server tests passing (`tests/test_species_feats.py`).
- **8b species variant choice.** `official_source._race_variants_from_5etools` builds `race["variants"]` (always a list) from `_versions`: explicit versions that REPLACE a base trait (`_mod.entries` mode replaceArr; name = text after "; ", e.g. "Drow Lineage"; id = lowercase-hyphen slug) and abstract versions (Dragonborn: name from the abstract template, resist from the implementation or its `_variables`). Variant dicts: `{id, name, speed, darkvision, resist, innate_spells, skill_proficiencies, removes}` (`removes` = data keys the version sets to null). 13 races have variants (Aasimar/MPMM 3, Custom Lineage 2, Elf/XPHB 3, Gnome/XPHB 2, Goliath/XPHB 6, Kobold/MPMM 3, Shifter EFA and MPMM 4 each, Tiefling/XPHB 3, Dragonborn/XPHB 10, Dragonborn Chromatic/Gem/Metallic FTD 5 each). Aetherborn/PSK's lone version only appends a trait (appendArr), so it is not a choice; the plan's count of 17 included three `_copy` Lorwyn races the converter skips; its "no `_versions` but 2+ named additionalSpells" fallback matches no real record (Astral Elf's entries are unnamed) and is not implemented.
- `species:<race>:variant` ("Lineage / Ancestry", count 1, blocking until picked). The selected variant adds its `innate_spells` (a race with variants drops the base entries tagged with a lineage name, so an unpicked Elf/Tiefling has none), its `resist` to `damageModifiers.resistances`, its `speed` to the walk speed (Wood Elf 35; subrace still overrides) and its `darkvision` to `sheet.senses` (higher wins). `sheet.species.variant = {id, name} | null`; the Markdown/PDF species line appends " - <variant name>". Not modelled: variants' `known` cantrips (Dancing Lights, Druidcraft, Thaumaturgy, ...), the spellcasting-ability choice, and trait text (the sheet still lists the base lineage trait with all alternatives).
- **8c resistance choice: already done in Slice 3** (`species:<race>:resistance` from `resist_options`, verified by `test_dragonborn_resist_choice_is_offered_and_applied`). The one interaction: when a race's variants carry `resist` (Dragonborn/XPHB and FTD colours, Tiefling/XPHB legacies) the variant decides the resistance and the separate resistance choice is no longer emitted; Dragonborn/PHB and Reborn/RHW keep it. `test_xphb_tiefling_resist_choice` became `test_xphb_tiefling_resistance_is_decided_by_the_legacy_variant`.
- Tests: 944 server tests passing (`tests/test_species_variants.py`).
- **8d size choice.** Catalog races carry `size_options` (the mapped sizes when 5etools lists more than one, always `["Small","Medium"]`; `[]` otherwise); `size` stays the first value. 30 of the 144 non-copy races are multi-size (95 are Medium only, 18 Small only, one is "V"/varies and keeps the Medium fallback); no subrace has a size. `species:<race>:size` (count 1, option ids `small`/`medium`) is blocking until picked. `sheet.species.size` = the selected size, the race's own size when it has no options, `null` while unpicked. `derived.carryingCapacity` now reads selected size, else race size, else Medium (a test patches in a Tiny/Large option to prove the wiring, because Small and Medium share the x1 multiplier). `size` sits inside the existing `sheet.species` dict, so `export_fields` needed no new key; `types.ts` `SheetSpecies` gains `variant` and `size`.
- Tests: 950 server tests passing (`tests/test_species_size.py`).
- **8e version semantics (done, narrow).** A variant's `removes` list is applied: `skillProficiencies` drops the race's dedicated skill-choice trait (`...-trait-choice-skill-proficiency`; Custom Lineage "Darkvision", Kobold "Defiance" and "Draconic Sorcery"), `darkvision` ignores the race's own darkvision (Custom Lineage "Skill Proficiency"), `additionalSpells` ignores the race's innate spells (Kobold "Craftiness", "Defiance"; the base has only `known` cantrips, so nothing visible changes). Variant ids are the name after "; " slugged (`darkvision`, `skill-proficiency`, `craftiness`, `defiance`, `draconic-sorcery`), not the `custom-lineage-...` ids the plan guessed. A selection stored for a removed choice is reported in `pruned`. Not done: a variant that ADDS `skillProficiencies` (no real record does; the field is carried as `skill_proficiencies` on the variant but unused) and the `speed` removal key.
- Tests: 953 server tests passing.

## Slice 10 - ruleset, ASI double-dip, source picker
- **`draft.ruleset`** is `"2014" | "2024" | "mixed"`; missing means `"mixed"` (no warning), any other value means `"mixed"` plus warning `ruleset-invalid`. `emptyDraft.ruleset = "mixed"`; `DRAFT_VERSION` is now 2 and `mergeDraft` fills the key for older drafts (no separate migration step); `ruleset` and `sources` are plain draft keys, so they survive draft download/upload.
- **`resolve._asi_policy(ruleset, race, subrace, bg) -> (species_on, background_on, warnings)`**, applied right before the species/background ability passes. "Species ASI" is everything the species and subspecies grant: fixed `ability_bonuses` and the `species:<id>:ability-bonus` template (Half-Elf, VRGR lineages, Variant Human, subrace options); when off, both are skipped, and a stored selection for the missing template lands in `pruned`. 2014: background ASI off (warning `ruleset-2014-background-asi-ignored` if it had one). 2024: species ASI off (`ruleset-2024-species-asi-ignored`), plus `ruleset-2024-background-no-asi` / `ruleset-2024-background-no-origin-feat` for a background without them. Mixed: a background with `ability_bonus_options` plus a species with any ASI turns the species ASI off (`asi-double-dip-prevented`); otherwise both apply. Every ruleset conflict is a warning, never a problem. Species feats and lineages/variants are not touched by the policy (a feat's own ASI is a feat effect, not a species ASI).
- **Tagging**: records carry `ruleset` "2014" or "2024" (races/backgrounds from 5etools `edition: "one"`: 18 races = XPHB 10 + EFA 5 + RHW 3, 60 backgrounds; feats from `reference_data.RULESET_2024_SOURCES`; subspecies inherit the species; SRD records get "2014" in `loader._load_srd`). `reference_data.ruleset_allows(record, kind, ruleset)`: "2014" hides 2024 records of every kind, "2024" hides only 2014 backgrounds, "mixed" hides nothing. It only affects pickers (`/api/catalog` and the ASI-level and species-feat feat pools via `_feat_offered`); `resolve()` never rejects a species/background on ruleset alone, so a hidden selection keeps resolving. ASI-level eligibility is by ruleset and slot level (`_is_asi_eligible_feat`, see the A1 section at the end).
- **Sources**: `engine/resolve._source_allowed(record, sources)` is the one filter (species, subspecies, backgrounds, feats). Subspecies now carry `source` (`_subrace_from_5etools`), so `sources: ["phb"]` drops `dwarf-phb-duergar-mtf` with `unknown-subspecies` and no ASI from it; SRD records (no source) are always allowed. `GET /api/catalog?ruleset=&sources=phb,xge` filters species/subspecies/backgrounds, adds `ruleset`/`source` per record and a top-level `sources: [{id, name, count}]` built from the UNFILTERED catalog (count = species + subspecies + backgrounds + feats of that book; SRD not listed; names from `reference_data.SOURCE_NAMES`, fallback is the code).
- **Frontend**: `SET_RULESET` / `SET_SOURCES`; `CatalogProvider` reads the draft and refetches on ruleset+sources change (it also fetches the unfiltered list for names). Home has a ruleset `<select>` with a help line and a "Sources" `<details>` (one checkbox per book with count, "Всички" = `[]`, "Само SRD" = `["srd"]`; `[]` means everything, so unchecking the last box falls back to `["srd"]`). SpeciesPage/BackgroundPage list a selected-but-hidden record anyway (marked "скрит от текущите филтри") and show `ruleset-*` / `asi-double-dip` warnings. Browser-checked at 375px: no horizontal overflow, species list 153 -> 10 with SRD only.
- Tests: `tests/test_ruleset_sources.py`, `tests/test_ruleset_asi.py`; several `test_resolve.py` expectations changed because the default "mixed" now prevents the Human +1 / 2024-background double-dip and `sources:["phb"]` no longer allows MTF/ERLW subspecies.

## Slice 11 - sourcebook content import (spells first)
- **11.0 split (done).** `server/catalog/official_source.py` is now a re-export shim (every public and private name still importable from it, so loader, resolve and tests are unchanged). The code lives in the package `server/catalog/official/` by concern: `paths.py` (the only importer of `chargen.sources`, owns the `sys.path` fix, `_repo_root`, `official_dir`, `has_official`), `common.py` (`slug`, ability/speed/darkvision/senses/damage helpers), `equipment.py` (SRD item matching and starting-equipment envelopes), `proficiencies.py`, `languages.py`, `innate_spells.py` (innate-spell parsing, `_SPELL_SCHOOL_CODES`), `races.py` (+ `load_official_races`), `backgrounds.py` (+ `load_official_backgrounds`), `feats.py` (+ `load_official_feats`, `_parse_spell_filter`, feat spells). Import graph is acyclic: paths <- common <- equipment/proficiencies/languages/innate_spells <- races/feats <- backgrounds. Verified: the multiset of non-blank code lines is identical before and after, apart from the added imports. Older paragraphs above that cite `official_source.py` line numbers/sizes describe the pre-split file. Tests: 978 server, 396 root (+8 skipped) unchanged.
- **11a.1 spell data (done).** `chargen/sync.py --official` now also fetches `spells/index.json`, `spells/sources.json` and every `*.json` the index lists into `official_dir()/spells/` (`OFFICIAL_SUBDIR_TARGETS`; index entries that are not plain `name.json` strings are ignored, so a bad index cannot write outside the folder; status lists the files). `catalog/official/paths.py::spells_dir()` returns `$DND_5ETOOLS_DATA/spells` when that env var points at a 5etools `data` directory with `spells/index.json`, else `official_dir()/spells`, else `None` (`has_official_spells()`). `official/spells.py::load_raw_spells(dir)` / `load_spell_class_lookup(dir)` read the books and `sources.json` ([] / {} when missing; a broken file is skipped). Verified by script on the local copy: 17 books, 936 raw spells (XPHB 391, PHB 361, XGE 95, TCE 21, FRHoF 19, EGW 15, AI 7, FTD 7, SCC 5, BMT 3, LLK 3, AAG 2, IDRotF 2, SatO 2, AitFR-AVT 1, EFA 1, GGR 1), no `_copy`. Tests: `tests/test_spell_import.py` (spell-file tests skip without data); the local files were copied from `~/Documents/5etools-src/data/spells` into `~/.claude/dnd/.data/official/spells` (what the sync would fetch) so the default test run exercises them.
- **11a.2 spell converter, ids, ruleset/source-aware options (done).** `official/spells.py::_spell_from_5etools(raw, class_lookup, srd_indices, taken)` produces the SRD spell shape (`index, name, level, school{index,name}, casting_time (+ casting_condition for reactions), range, components, material, ritual, duration, concentration, desc[], higher_level[], classes[{index,name}], source, ruleset`), rich text flattened to plain paragraphs (tags like `{@spell x}`, `{@dc 15}`, `{@damage 8d6}` stripped; tables as " | " rows; the "At Higher Levels" heading dropped). `load_official_spells(dir, srd_indices)` converts every book; `loader._load_spells()` merges them: SRD records stay (tagged `source:"SRD"`, `ruleset:"2014"`), an official spell that is the same spell only appends its missing classes, everything else is added.
  - **ID rule**: 2024-ruleset books (`RULESET_2024_SOURCES`: XPHB, FRHoF, EFA, ...) always `slug-source` (`fireball-xphb`, `divine-smite-xphb`); other books use the bare slug, except an SRD spell (same slug, or one of the 17 renames in `SRD_SPELL_ALIASES`, e.g. `tensers-floating-disk` -> `floating-disk`) keeps the SRD id and a bare slug already taken gets `-source`. `Catalog.spell_aliases` (17 renames + 32 bare slugs of 2024-only spells such as `divine-smite` -> `divine-smite-xphb`) and `Catalog.resolve_spell_id(id)` (id, else alias, else None) are used by `_innate_entry_to_sheet` and the feat `from` lists. `Catalog.spell_twins` pairs the 2014 and 2024 version of the same spell (379 pairs; the 2024 books keep the old names such as "Bigby's Hand", so the renames are included).
  - **Visibility**: `resolve._spell_visible(catalog, spell, sources, ruleset)`: `_source_allowed` (SRD spells always pass) and `ruleset_allows(spell, "spell", ...)`; for a twin pair "mixed" offers the 2014 record, "2024" the 2024 record, "2014" hides 2024 spells, unless the other version's book is excluded by `sources` (then the visible one stays). `_spell_options`, `_spell_options_up_to`, `_spell_filter_options` and the feat spell templates take `sources`/`ruleset` from the draft; spells already selected in the owner's choices stay offered, so changing the ruleset never prunes a pick. Explicit feat `from` lists are not filtered by ruleset (resolved through aliases only).
  - **Verified numbers** (script over the raw files, cross-checked by an independent model in the scratchpad): catalog 936 spells = 319 SRD + 617 added (XPHB 391, XGE 95, PHB 42, TCE 21, FRHoF 19, EGW 15, AI 7, FTD 7, SCC 5, BMT 3, LLK 3, AAG 2, IDRotF 2, SatO 2, AitFR-AVT 1, EFA 1, GGR 1); 411 are 2024-ruleset; 302 of the 361 PHB spells match an SRD slug and 17 more match through the renames (5etools prints `srd: "<SRD name>"` on exactly those 17), leaving 42 new; visible unfiltered: mixed 557, 2014 525, 2024 557. Distinct innate-spell ids with no SRD match: 13; with the import 12 resolve and only `battle-familiar` (an Unearthed Arcana spell) stays null. 16 spells (EGW 15, GGR Encode Thoughts) have no class data (`classes: []`).
  - **Deviation from the plan**: class lists read `sources.json` `class` AND `classVariant`. All 95 XGE spells (and the new TCE/FTD/BMT ones) keep their class lists only under `classVariant`, so following the plan literally left 135 spells without a class (every XGE spell unusable for class pickers); with the union only the 16 above remain.
  - Several `test_resolve.py` option counts rose (script-verified, conditional on the spell files being present): e.g. wizard Magic Initiate cantrips 14 -> 16, level 1 27 -> 30.
- **11a.3 /api/spells slimming, detail endpoint, SpellLibrary (done).** `/api/spells` rows are slim (`id, name, level, school, castingTime, range, components, duration, concentration, ritual, source, ruleset`; no description or material), accept `?ruleset=&sources=` next to `class/level/q`, and use `resolve._spell_visible` (default mixed = one record per spell). `app.py` builds `_spells_by_class` (class id -> name-sorted spells, `""` = all) and the row dicts once at import. `GET /api/spells/<id>` returns the row plus `material`, `desc[]`, `higherLevel[]`, `castingCondition`, `classes[{id,name}]`, `sourceName`; ids resolve through `Catalog.resolve_spell_id` (so `tensers-floating-disk` and `divine-smite` work), 404 JSON otherwise. Sizes: `class=wizard` 369 spells = 89 KB (was 233 KB with SRD only, descriptions included), `class=cleric` 32 KB, all spells 134 KB, about 1 ms per request. Frontend: `useSpells(classId, level, query, ruleset, sources)` passes the draft's values; `CatalogSpell.desc` is gone, `SpellDetail`/`fetchSpellDetail` added; `SpellLibrary` cards are `<details>` and fetch the detail once on first open (retry on error). Checked in the browser pane at 375px (Warlock: Hex offered in the picker, card expands with text, no horizontal overflow).
- **Disclosed**: the Sources picker still counts only species/subspecies/backgrounds/feats (spell-only books are not listed); 16 EGW/GGR spells have `classes: []`; `SpellLibrary` still lists only cantrips and level 1 (unchanged).

## Feat skill-or-tool picks (Skilled, 2026-10-09)
- **Real data**: `skillToolLanguageProficiencies` appears on exactly 2 of 305 feats (Skilled/PHB, Skilled/XPHB), both `[{"choose": [{"count": 3, "from": ["anySkill", "anyTool"]}]}]`; 0 of 160 races; 1 of 161 backgrounds (Custom Background/PHB, a list of fixed alternatives `{anyLanguage: 2}` / `{anyLanguage:1, anyTool:1}` / `{anyTool:1}`, NOT converted). No real record uses a language variant.
- **Model**: `official/feats._skill_tool_choices_from_5etools` turns each `choose` group into a `proficiency_choices` envelope appended after the ordinary ones. `from` with both -> new `choices.py` option set `any_skill_or_tool` (18 skills `skill-*` then 31 tools, 49 options, ids unique); lone anySkill/anyTool reuse `any_skill`/`any_tool`; a `from` naming anyLanguage is skipped. Cids are the existing `feat:<index>:proficiency-choice-<i>` (background grant) or origin-scoped (`species:<id>:feat-<i>:feat:skilled-xphb:proficiency-choice-0`, ASI `class:<c>:<lvl>:asi:feat:<idx>:proficiency-choice-<i>`); label "Skilled: Skill or Tool Proficiency". Skills land in `sheet.proficiencies.skills` and tools in `tools` through the existing feat proficiency-choice merge sites; no resolve changes besides the label.
- **Reachability**: Skilled is category '' (PHB) / `O` (XPHB), and only `G` feats are ASI-eligible, so it cannot be taken at an ASI level in real data; the ASI cid shape is covered at template level. It is reachable as a species feat (Human/XPHB) and via background grants (Charlatan, Noble, Scribe XPHB; Archaeologist/EFA; Chondathan Freebooter/FRHoF; Rewarded and Ruined/BMT).
- **Limits**: like every other feat proficiency choice, options are not disabled for skills the character already has; a duplicate pick is accepted and the sheet lists the skill once.
- Frontend: no change (generic cards; ~49 checkboxes in one list). Tests: 1044 server tests passing (`tests/test_skilled_feat.py`, 15).

## Slice 11b - non-SRD subclasses
- **11b.1 data plumbing (done).** `chargen/sync.py --official` also fetches `class/index.json` plus every class file it lists (`OFFICIAL_SUBDIR_TARGETS`, same unsafe-name guard as spells) and `optionalfeatures.json` (`OFFICIAL_TARGETS`). `catalog/official/paths.py`: `class_dir()` / `has_official_classes()` (`DND_5ETOOLS_DATA/class` first, then `official_dir()/class`) and `optionalfeatures_file()`; re-exported from `official_source.py`. `official/subclasses.py`: `load_raw_class_data(dir) -> (subclasses, subclass_features)` and `load_raw_optional_features(path)`. Verified by script: 15 class files, 315 raw subclasses, 1397 raw subclass features, 213 optional features. For the default local test run the class files and `optionalfeatures.json` were copied from `~/Documents/5etools-src/data` into `~/.claude/dnd/.data/official/` (a write outside the repo; the sync would fetch the same files). Tests: `tests/test_subclass_import.py`, `tests/test_chargen_sync.py`.
- **11b.2 converter, catalog merge, row index (done).** `official/subclasses.py::convert_subclasses(raw_subs, raw_feats, srd_ids, resolve_spell) -> (subclasses, features, level_rows)` and `subclass_twins_from(raw_subs, known_ids)`. Filters: `_copy` dropped, class must be one of the 12 SRD classes with classSource PHB/XPHB, `srd: true` records dropped (the SRD record stays). Subclass id `slug(strip trailing "(...)" of shortName)-source` (`hexblade-xge`, `totem-warrior-phb`, `berserker-xphb`), duplicates/SRD clashes raise. Record `{index, name (raw, e.g. "The Hexblade"), short_name, class, source, ruleset (edition "one" = 2024), desc: [], spells: []}`. A subclass's features are its `subclassFeatures` refs expanded depth-first into `refSubclassFeature` children in document order (blank class/subclass source = PHB); children inside an `options` block are inlined as text (optional-feature refs become a bare name line; deviation from the plan's empty named block, which `_flatten_entries` would drop); feature id `{subId}-{level}-{slug(name)}`, records carry `class` and `subclass` refs so the existing subclass gating works. `loader._merge_official_subclasses` tags SRD subclasses `source:"SRD"`, `ruleset:"2014"`, merges the rest, and sets `Catalog.subclass_twins` (symmetric, from `reprintedAs` only; an `srd:true` record is its bare SRD id). `Catalog.class_level_row/subclass_level_row` are dict lookups on a lazily built `_row_index` (rebuilt when `len(levels)` changes, so hand-built catalogs still work): 50 resolves of a level-20 Fighter went from 0.157 s to 0.047 s.
  - **Verified numbers** (script with the project `slug`, then asserted in `tests/test_subclass_import.py`): 315 raw subclasses (124 `_copy`), 176 non-`_copy` in scope = 12 SRD + 164 imported (2014: PHB 28, XGE 31, TCE 26, SCAG 6, PSA 5, EGW 3, DMG 2, FTD 2, VRGR 2, BGG 1, DSotDQ 1, PSK 1 = 108; 2024: XPHB 48, FRHoF 8 = 56); 1397 raw features -> 1089 imported feature records and 671 imported level rows (catalog: 1496 features, 961 level rows); 48 twin pairs (96 map entries); no id clashes.
  - Existing SRD drafts (all 12 SRD subclasses at levels 1/3/9/20) resolve identically to the pre-change output and to an SRD-only copy of the catalog (tested). `test_loader` counts are conditional on the class files being present.
- **11b.3 API and ClassPage subclass list (done).** `resolve._subclass_visible(catalog, sub, sources, ruleset)` is `_spell_visible` over `catalog.subclass_twins` (mixed shows the 2014 half of a pair, 2024 the 2024 half, 2014 hides 2024 records; the other half stays offered when its book is excluded by `sources`; a subclass without a twin is visible under every ruleset except 2014-hides-2024). `GET /api/catalog/class/<id>?ruleset=&sources=&include=<subclassId>` now returns `subclasses: [{index, name, source, ruleset, hidden?}]` (SRD first, then by name; `include` adds the draft's selection with `hidden: true` when the filters hide it; other class fields unchanged). `_catalog_sources` also counts subclasses (DSotDQ, PSK, PSA... now appear in the Sources picker). Verified visible totals across the 12 classes: 2014 120, mixed 128, 2024 128 (Cleric 19 under each; Warlock 9/9/9; Fighter 10/10/10), asserted per class in `tests/test_subclass_api.py`. Frontend `ClassPage.tsx` fetches with the draft's `ruleset`, `sources` and `include=subclassId` (re-fetches when any changes), labels options `Name (SOURCE)` for non-SRD and " — скрит от текущите филтри" for a hidden pick; native `<select>` (at most 24 options).
- **11b.4 subclass spells (done).** The converter fills `subclasses[id]["spells"]` in the SRD entry shape plus an explicit `mode`: `prepared` and `known` string lists keyed by class level -> `mode "always"`, `expanded` -> `mode "expanded"`; a warlock-only `sN` key (spell level N) -> warlock level `2N-1`. Names are cleaned (`toll the dead|xge`, `guidance#c`); a 2014 subclass resolves `slug` through `Catalog.resolve_spell_id`, a 2024 subclass tries `slug-xphb` first. `_subclass_spell_ids` now uses each entry's `mode` (falling back to `SUBCLASS_SPELL_MODE`, then "always"), so one subclass can carry both lists. **Skipped (disclosed)**: object values (`{all}`, `{choose}`, `{"_": ...}`), `innate` and daily/ritual/resource blocks, and every block of a subclass with several named blocks (Giant BGG, Land XPHB, Arcane Archer, Divine Soul, Genie). Verified: 109 `additionalSpells` records in scope; string refs prepared 527 + expanded 114 + known 95 = 736, 0 unresolved (`unresolved_spell_refs`); the 98 single-block imported subclasses with spells emit 661 entries, 81 of them from string lists, no spell listed twice. Cantrips from a `known` list (e.g. Light's `light`) are treated like any other always-prepared spell. Existing SRD snapshots unchanged.
- **11b.5 fixed proficiencies (done).** `reference_data.SUBCLASS_PROFICIENCIES` grew from 1 to 19 subclasses (Life plus valor-phb/xphb, swords-xge (medium armor + `scimitars`), death-dmg, nature-phb, tempest-phb, war-phb, solidarity-psa, strength-psa, zeal-psa, order-tce, twilight-tce, forge-xge (heavy armor + `smiths-tools`), rune-knight-tce, assassin-phb/xphb (`disguise-kit`, `poisoners-kit`), hexblade-xge, bladesinging-tce), each read off the imported feature text; the grant is applied the moment the subclass is selected (no level gate, so a 2024 subclass picked at class level 1 already grants it). **Not modelled**: skill/tool/weapon choices (Lore, Knowledge, Cavalier, Samurai, Kensei, Order's skills), Mastermind (disguise + forgery kit, plus a gaming set choice), Mercy (herbalism kit, Insight/Medicine), Drunken Master's Performance, Bladesinger FRHoF's melee-weapon list and Bladesinging's one-handed-weapon pick. Tests: Hexblade longsword `proficient`, Tempest chain mail gives no `armor-not-proficient` warning (Light does), Swords scimitar, Forge smith's tools.
- **11b.6 optionalfeatureProgression picks (done).** `official/subclasses.py::convert_optional_features` turns the optional features of featureTypes `MV:B` (43), `FS:B` (2), `AS` (8), `RN` (6), `ED` (17) = 76 records into `opt-{slug}-{source}` catalog features `{index,name,desc,source,ruleset,prerequisite_level}` (11 carry a level prerequisite: Rune Knight 2 at 7, Four Elements 9). `convert_subclasses(..., raw_options)` attaches `feature_specific.optional_progression = {progression, options}` to the subclass's first feature at the first progression level (6 subclasses: Battle Master PHB 23 options / XPHB 20, Arcane Archer 8, Rune Knight 6, Four Elements 17, Swords 2); a 2014 subclass is offered only 2014-source options, a 2024 one only 2024-source. `resolve._optional_progression_template` builds cid `class:{cls}:{lvl}:feature:{featureId}` with `count_by_level: True` (so level-down is `pruned`, not a problem) and marks options above the class level `disabled` with `disabledReason`. `opt-dueling-phb`/`opt-two-weapon-fighting-phb` are in `CLASS_FEATURE_EFFECTS`. The loader counts 407 + 1089 + 76 features. The Four Elements `required` Elemental Attunement is modelled since S3.
- **11b disclosed skips.** `_copy` subclasses/features (pure duplicates); (Eldritch Knight/Arcane Trickster slots, cantrips and known spells are done since PART 2; Psi Warrior/Soulknife dice are done since R6, see "Class resources - R6"); `featProgression`; feature-text `options` choices other than the four build-time ones now done in S4 (per-rest choices such as The Third Eye and Master Transmuter, and grant-all blocks such as Psionic Power, stay text only); choose-type/innate/daily/ritual additional spells and every block of multi-block subclasses (Land XPHB, Divine Soul, Genie, Giant, Arcane Archer); skill/weapon choice grants; feature mechanics (effects, HP/AC formulas); (the 2024 subclass unlock level is done, see PART 3).
## Slice 11c - mundane items
- **11c (done).** `python3 -m chargen.sync --official` also pulls `items-base.json` (`OFFICIAL_TARGETS`; `paths.items_base_file()`, override first). Script counts over the raw file: 230 base items, 125 of weapon/armor type (M 60, R 38, LA 6, MA 11, HA 8, S 2 by raw `type`; sources XPHB 51, PHB 50, DMG 10, XDMG 8, others 6); 101 are SRD (`srd` 50 / `srd52` 51); of the other 24, 16 are modern/futuristic firearms with their XDMG reprints (skipped on `age`) and 3 have rarity `unknown` (Hooked Shortspear, Light Repeating Crossbow, Spiked Armor), leaving 5 imported (the 2024-reprint rule is unit-tested only, nothing real reaches it): Double-Bladed Scimitar, Hoopak, Musket, Pistol, Yklwa. `items.json` (magic items) is not read. `official/items.py::convert_items` maps the SRD item shape (category, damage, range/throw_range, properties, armor class, cost in gp/sp/cp) plus `source`/`ruleset`; items whose name matches an SRD item (`_find_srd_equipment_item`, e.g. Light Crossbow) are skipped. `loader._merge_official_items` tags SRD items `source: SRD` and merges (SRD wins). `choices.equipment_category_options` ignores non-SRD items so Slice 6 starting-equipment option sets are unchanged. `/api/items` rows carry `source` and `ruleset` and accept `?ruleset=&sources=`; `useItems` passes the draft's values. Disclosed: mastery, reload, burst-fire and renaissance-age flavour are not modelled; the armor path is unit-tested only (no non-SRD armor survives the filters); the Hoopak's alternate ranged mode is text only.


## Round-2 audit, Slice B - frontend correctness (2026-10-09)
- F6: `ChoicePicker` no longer builds the next selection from the last resolution. It calls `onToggle(choiceId, optionId, count)`; the reducer action `TOGGLE_CHOICE_OPTION` computes the new array from the draft's stored selection (`storedSelection`), so two taps inside the 150 ms debounce both register. Overflow drops the oldest pick (as before). `SET_CHOICE_SELECTION` still exists.
- F7: `mergeDraft` runs `sanitizeDraft` (drops non-object/idless class entries and bad `equipment.added` entries, clamps levels, dedupes classIds); `ClassBlock` checks `r.ok` before `setClassDetail`; `components/ErrorBoundary.tsx` wraps the app in `main.tsx` (offers "download raw draft" and "reset").
- F12: `components/CommitNumberInput.tsx` (local string state, commits on blur/Enter, clamps) used for class level and manual HP rolls (max = hit die).
- F13: standard-array selects list all six values; choosing a value held by another ability swaps the two.

## Round-2 audit, Slice C - visible problems and gates (2026-10-09)
- F5: `frontend/src/problems.ts` maps blocking problem ids to wizard steps (`stepForProblem`); `components/StepProblems.tsx` renders them under each step's `<h1>` and `StepNav` adds them to the badge count (one step per problem; unmapped ids stay Export-only). The draft reducer caps the whole character at 20 levels (`MAX_TOTAL_LEVEL`): `SET_CLASS_LEVEL` clamps, `ADD_CLASS` is a no-op at 20, the ClassPage level input's `max` and the add-class buttons follow. A loaded draft that is already over the cap still resolves and shows `total-level-exceeded`.
- F9: `_pending_export_problems` (server/app.py) adds `export-missing-class|species|background` when `sheet.classes/species/background` are empty (gate only; resolve problems unchanged). ExportPage mirrors it with `missingParts`.
- F10: `/api/catalog` returns `backgroundsFallback`; under ruleset 2024 with no visible background (e.g. sources `srd`, or `phb,xge,tce`) the list falls back to the source-allowed SRD background (Acolyte). BackgroundPage shows a note; resolve already warns that a 2014 background grants no 2024 ASI/Origin feat.

## Round-2 audit, Slice D - resolve consistency (2026-10-09)
- F3 (policy: warn, don't block): a species / subspecies / background hidden only by `draft.sources` now resolves like a ruleset-hidden one and adds the warning `source-hidden-species|subspecies|background` (shown on the Species/Background pages). `unknown-*` problems remain only for ids absent from the full catalog, and `subspecies-mismatch` is never emitted when the species itself is unknown. The old "reject like unknown" tests in test_resolve.py / test_ruleset_sources.py were rewritten.
- F4: a subclass below `SUBCLASS_UNLOCK_LEVEL` for its class is ignored everywhere (features, proficiencies, spells, sheet) and a `pruned` entry `class:<id>:subclass` is added (resolve.py per-class loop).
- F14: `SUBCLASS_SPELLCASTING_ABILITY` (reference_data.py) gives the 1/3-caster subclasses and monk Shadow/Elements an ability, so their `sheet.spells[i].spellSaveDC/spellAttackBonus` are no longer null. Eldritch Knight still has no sheet.spells entry (no spell choices/always-prepared spells in the data); third-caster slots remain a known gap.
- F11: manual HP rolls outside 1..hit die add `hp-roll-invalid-<class>-<level>` warnings (still replaced by the average); ClassPage shows them and offers a level-1 roll input for non-primary classes.

## Round-2 audit, Slice F - API hardening (2026-10-09)
- F15: `app.config["MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH"] = 1_000_000` (oversized bodies get the JSON 413 from the error handler); `resolve()` caps `pruned` at `MAX_PRUNED_REPORTED` (200) and appends a `pruned-more` entry carrying the remainder count (subclass-unlock entries are ordered first so the cap never hides them). HomePage keys pruned rows by index+id and has "Изчисти отпаднали избори" (reducer `CLEAR_STALE_SELECTIONS`: removes stored selections whose choice id is not in the current resolution, and `subclassId` for `class:<id>:subclass` entries).

## Round-2 audit, Slice E - phone pickers (F8, 2026-10-09)
- `ChoiceOption` gained optional `group?: string` and `hiddenByFilters?: boolean` (backward compatible). The server sets `group = "spell-level-N"` on class spell options (`_tag_spell_level_groups` in resolve.py, cantrips and spells-known); other choices omit it.
- `ChoicePicker`: search input (case-insensitive label match; selected options always shown) when a choice has more than 20 options (`FILTER_THRESHOLD`); consecutive options sharing `group` get a heading via `groupLabel` (spell-level-0 "Трикове", spell-level-N "Заклинания ниво N", asi/general/origin/epic-boon/feat-2014/any-school/school mapped to Bulgarian, unknown strings shown raw); disabled or `hiddenByFilters` options that are not selected sit behind "Показване на недостъпните (N)".
- `ChoiceCard`: new `defaultExpanded` prop (pages pass `choice.id === firstPendingId(list)`; per page/tab only the first pending card opens, resolved cards start collapsed); header shows title + "избрани X/N". `SpeciesPage` has a name search box (selected species always listed).
- Measured at 375px, fresh Wizard 20 (features tab / spells tab): checkbox rows 581 / 369 -> 6 / 31; DOM nodes 2159 / 2231 -> 180 / 1217 (rest is SpellLibrary); scroll height 30970 / 19632 px -> 1890 / 3407 px; scrollWidth 375 (no horizontal overflow). Species list 153 -> 9 for "elf".

## ASI feat eligibility, third casters, 2024 overlays - PART 1 (2026-10-09)
Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-09-feat-eligibility-third-casters-2024-overlays.md`. Decisions U1-U9 (orchestrator defaults, user may override): U1 Unearthed Arcana `G` feats stay in the 2014 pool; U2 under 2024 the 2014 category-less feats are NOT offered (`mixed` has them); U3 Fighting Style feats are not offered at ASI levels yet (disclosed; RESOLVED by C3 on 2026-10-10: they are offered under 2024/mixed to characters with a Fighting Style feature, see "2024 chassis - C3" below); U4 Epic Boons only at slot level 19 (the multiclass RAW case, a character level 19 reached with a class level below 19, is not modelled; disclosed); U5/U7 cross-edition "same name = already taken" (slice A5) was skipped at first and is now implemented as S2 (see "Class resources ... S2" below); U8 third casters add `level // 3` in multiclass for both editions (Part 2, built); U9 2024 overlays apply only under ruleset "2024", not "mixed" (Part 3, built).
- **A1 eligibility by ruleset and slot level (done).** `_is_asi_eligible_feat(feat, ruleset="mixed", slot_level=4)`; slot level = the class level of the ASI choice. Rule table (categories are 5etools codes; `''` = category-less 2014 books):

  | ruleset | categories offered | Epic Boon (`EB`) |
  |---|---|---|
  | `2014` | `''`, `G` (G after `ruleset_allows` = the Unearthed Arcana feats) | never |
  | `2024` | `G`, `O` (+ `FS` when the character has a Fighting Style feature, C3; `FS:P` / `FS:R` are NOT admitted, F1) | slot level >= 19 |
  | `mixed` / unknown | `''`, `G`, `O` (+ `FS`, same condition, C3/F1) | slot level >= 19 |

  Always excluded: `ability-score-improvement-xphb` (the app's own ASI combos), and categories `D`, `DG`; `FS`, `FS:P`, `FS:R` are excluded unless `_HeldFeatState.fighting_style` (C3, never under 2014). `_asi_feat_pool(catalog, sources, ruleset, slot_level)` = eligible AND `_feat_offered`; `_asi_choice_templates` builds the pool once per "slot < 19" / "slot >= 19" and `_resolve_asi_selected_feats` honours a pick with the same two checks. `_species_feat_pool` is unchanged (its grant rules are separate).
- **Verified numbers** (script over the real catalog; match the plan): 305 feats = `''` 108, G 95, O 35, EB 32, D 13, DG 9, FS 11, FS:P 1, FS:R 1. Pool sizes (no sources): 2014 slot 4/19 = 123/123; 2024 = 129/161; mixed = 237/269 (each +21 ASI combos). With sources (2014/2024/mixed): `["srd"]` 0/0/0; `["srd","xphb"]` 0/52/52 at slot 4 and 64 at slot 19 (2024/mixed); `["phb"]` 42/0/42; `["phb","xge","tce"]` 72/0/72. Options whose name occurs twice: 92 under mixed, 0 under 2014 and 2024.
- **Timing**: `resolve()` for a level-20 Fighter (human/acolyte, mixed): 0.97 ms before, 1.6 ms after (mean of 20; limit 50 ms).
- Tests: `tests/test_asi_feat_eligibility.py` (11); `test_resolve.py` counts `94` -> `237`, `test_ruleset_asi.py` asserts a PHB feat is offered under 2014. Server suite 1160 passing.
- **Disclosed gap**: feats with prerequisites the ASI gate cannot check yet (race, spellcasting, spellcastingFeature) were offered enabled until slice A4 (done, see A4 below).
- **A2 an already-selected feat stays offered and warns (done, F2).** `_asi_pick_honored(feat, sources, ruleset, lvl)` = in the filtered pool OR structurally eligible under the permissive "mixed" rules (which is a superset of every ruleset's pool); the option template and `_resolve_asi_selected_feats` both use it, so a ruleset/sources change never silently drops a chosen feat. `_asi_choice_templates(..., class_choices=)` reads the stored single `feat:` pick (`_stored_asi_feat_pick`, same dedupe as the ASI walk) and appends it with `hiddenByFilters: true` when it is outside the pool; resolve adds the warning `feat-hidden-by-filters-<asi cid>` ("X is outside the current ruleset/sources but stays selected."). A stored `feat:` pick at an ASI cid that is not among that card's options (structurally ineligible there, e.g. an Epic Boon below level 19 or a Fighting Style feat) lands in `pruned` with "'X' can't be taken at this Ability Score Improvement and was ignored." Species feat grants: `_species_feat_pool(..., keep=)` keeps a stored id whose category fits the grant (`O` grant -> Origin; `None` grant -> `''`/G/O), the option gets `hiddenByFilters`, and the same warning id is emitted with the species cid. Disclosed: a kept feat is also applied when its book is excluded by `sources` (that is the point), and a hidden pick still takes its "already taken"/prerequisite checks like any option.
- Tests: `tests/test_asi_feat_kept.py` (7); `test_asi_feat_options_honor_draft_sources` now asserts the srd-only pick is kept (CHA 10, feat in sheet, warning) and 237 unrestricted options; `test_ruleset_asi.py::test_2014_keeps_selected_2024_asi_feat`. Server suite 1167.
- **A3 grouping and ordering for phones (done).** Server: `_feat_options(feats, ruleset)` builds feat options for both the ASI cards and species feat grants: `group` = `epic-boon` (EB) / `general` (G) / `origin` (O) / `feat-2014` (`''`); ASI combos get `group: "asi"` and stay first. Group order: 2014 `feat-2014, general`; otherwise `epic-boon, general, origin, feat-2014`; inside a group sorted by `(name, source)`; disabled options keep their place. The label becomes `Name (SOURCE)` only when the name occurs twice in that card (92 options under mixed, 0 under 2014, 2024 and slot 19 of 2024). Verified first feat ids: mixed slot 4 `abjuration-adept-au, actor-xphb, athlete-xphb`; 2014 `aberrant-dragonmark-erlw, actor-phb, adept-of-the-black-robes-dsotdq`; 2024 slot 19 `boon-of-blazing-dawn-abh, boon-of-bloodshed-frhof, boon-of-bountiful-health-frhof`. Frontend: only the missing piece was added to the existing `ChoicePicker` (Slice E already had group headings, the >20 filter and the collapsed unavailable list): a selected option flagged `hiddenByFilters` shows " — скрит от текущите филтри". Two older tests changed on purpose: the species "any pool" test now checks group order then name/source (it asserted plain label order), and the "non-spell options have no group" test excludes the ASI card (its options now carry `asi`/feat groups).
- Browser check (375px, Fighter 4, human/acolyte): 2014 card shows headings "Увеличение на характеристики", "Черти (2014)", "Общи черти"; search "alert" under mixed lists "Alert (XPHB)" under "Черти за произход" and "Alert (PHB)" under "Черти (2014)"; 2024 shows asi/general/origin headings; a stored `actor-xphb` under 2014 stays checked with the hidden-by-filters note; scrollWidth stays 375 in all.
- Tests: `tests/test_asi_feat_groups.py` (5). Server suite 1172.
- **A4 prerequisites for the newly eligible 2014 feats (done).** `_HeldFeatState` gained `race_names`, `subrace_names`, `size`, `can_cast_spell` (set once where `held` is built: species name lowercased with a trailing " (...)" stripped; subspecies name lowercased plus every `-` token of its index, so `elf-phb-drow-phb` matches "drow"; the species size from `_species_size`; `can_cast_spell` = Spellcasting/Pact Magic feature OR any fixed innate species spell). `_ASI_GATE_PREREQUISITE_KEYS` now also holds `race`, `spellcasting`, `spellcastingFeature` (`_STRUCTURED_FEAT_PREREQUISITE_KEYS` is untouched, so the background second pass is unchanged). A `race` alternative needs a non-empty list of dicts with a `name`; an entry is met when its name is a held race name (the entry name gets the same " (...)" stripping), `small race` is met by size Small, and an entry with `subrace` also needs it in `subrace_names`. Reasons: "a {Race} character" (or the entry's `displayEntry`), "the ability to cast at least one spell", "the Spellcasting feature". Verified by script: 18 feats carry `race` (all category `''`), 3 `spellcasting: true` (War Caster, Spell Sniper, Elemental Adept PHB), 2 `spellcastingFeature` (Cartomancer BMT with level 4; Rune Shaper BGG whose second alternative is a `background` key, so that feat stays ungated by the all-or-nothing rule); a Human Fighter 4 under 2014 has 17 of the 18 race-gated feats disabled (Prodigy lists Human).
- **Disclosed (U6 and others)**: a 2024 Elf's Drow lineage is a choice, not a subrace, so `drow-high-magic-xge` is falsely disabled for `elf-xphb` under mixed; a species' trait-granted spell *choices* (`_species_trait_spell_choices`) do not count toward `can_cast_spell`; Cartomancer/Rune Shaper's "Spellcasting feature" is the same flag as the other spellcasting prerequisites (any class with spell slots), and third-caster subclasses (Part 2) are not counted yet; "Vampire (Ixalan)" prerequisites compare as "vampire" (matches the catalog's `vampire-psz`).
- Tests: `tests/test_feat_prereq_race_spellcasting.py` (8). Server suite 1180; root suite 405 passed, 8 skipped. `resolve()` for a level-20 Fighter after A4: about 2.7 ms (limit 50 ms).

## Third-caster subclasses (EK, AT) - PART 2 (2026-10-09)
- **B1 converter (done).** `convert_subclasses` adds `record["spellcasting"] = {ability "INT", progression "1/3", list_class, kind "known"|"prepared", cantrips[20], spells[20], slots[20][4]}` when the raw subclass has `casterProgression: "1/3"` (`_third_caster_block`; `list_class` = first `class=X` in an `additionalSpells[*].expanded` `{all: ...}` entry, fallback `wizard`; `kind` = `known` when `spellsKnownProgression` exists (PHB), else `prepared` (XPHB)). Verified by script: exactly 4 in-scope records qualify (Eldritch Knight and Arcane Trickster, PHB and XPHB), all four share the same tables: cantrips `[0,0,2x7,3x11]`, spells `[0,0,3,4,4,4,5,6,6,7,8,8,9,10,10,11,11,11,12,13]`, slots lv3 `[2]`, 4-6 `[3]`, 7-9 `[4,2]`, 10-12 `[4,3]`, 13-15 `[4,3,2]`, 16-18 `[4,3,3]`, 19-20 `[4,3,3,1]`. `reference_data.SUBCLASS_SPELL_SCHOOL_LIMITS` holds the 2014 school limits (EK abjuration/evocation, AT enchantment/illusion, `free_at` [3,8,14,20]). Tests in `tests/test_subclass_import.py` (4 new).
- **B3 resolve integration (done).** `_effective_caster(catalog, rc)` -> `(ability, spellcasting dict, list_class_id)`: a class with its own spellcasting uses its level row; otherwise a subclass with a `spellcasting` block at class level >= 3 gives `INT` and a SYNTHETIC dict `{cantrips_known, spells_known, spells_kind, spell_slots_level_N}` built from the subclass tables (passed only to `_spell_choice_templates` as `{"spellcasting": ...}`; `rc["level_row"]` is untouched). `_spell_choice_templates(..., list_class_id=)` filters spell options on the wizard list for EK/AT but keeps the cids `class:<cls>:spells-cantrips` / `:spells-known` (so the count-variable pruning and `sheet.spells` work unchanged); label "Prepared Spells" when `spells_kind == "prepared"` (XPHB), "Spells Known" for PHB; description "... from the Wizard spell list". The `always_prepared` disable now also applies to the cantrip card and treats a spell's `spell_twins` entry as the same spell (AT XPHB under mixed: `mage-hand` is disabled although the subclass grants `mage-hand-xphb`). `sheet.spells` ability comes from the helper; `has_spellcasting_feature` (pre-walk) and `spell_slots_for` pass the subclass, so EK/AT now count as spellcasters for the feat prerequisites too: EK 4 under 2014 can take `feat:war-caster-phb`, Champion 4 cannot. Verified option counts (wizard list, mixed / 2014): level 3 cantrips 31 / 30, spells at max slot level 1: 43 / 41; level 7 (max slot level 2): 107 / 101 (all match the plan; 2024 equals mixed). EK 3 INT 16: DC 13, attack +5. Tests: `tests/test_third_casters.py` (12 resolve tests added; 30 total).
- **B4 2014 school limits (done).** `_spell_choice_templates(..., school_limit=, problems_out=)` (the school table is `reference_data.SUBCLASS_SPELL_SCHOOL_LIMITS`, only for the PHB subclasses; XPHB has no limit): `free = len([l for l in free_at if l <= class level])` (1 at 3-7, 2 at 8-13, 3 at 14-19, 4 at 20); options in the subclass schools get `group: "school"`, the rest `"any-school"` (school options sorted first so the picker headings "Школа на магията" / "Всяка школа" stay consecutive; this replaces the spell-level headings on that one card); once the stored picks already hold `free` outside spells the unselected outside options are disabled with "only N spell(s) may come from outside X or Y at this level"; more outside picks than `free` after a level-down adds the problem `subclass-spell-school-limit-<class>`. Verified (2014, wizard list): 15 abjuration/evocation spells of 41 at slot level 1 (26 of 101 at level 2), 9 enchantment/illusion for AT. The cantrip card has no school limit (RAW limits spells known only).
- **B5 pruning (done, tests only).** The generic count-variable pruning already covered it, so the new tests passed without code changes: EK 8 -> 3 with 6 stored known spells keeps the first 3 and reports "now allows 3 selection(s)" in `pruned`; switching to Champion lands both stored `class:fighter:spells-*` keys in `pruned` ("no longer applies"). Tests in `tests/test_third_casters.py` (38 total). Server suite 1222.

## 2024 overlays - PART 3 (2026-10-09)
- **O2 subclass unlock level by ruleset (done).** `reference_data.subclass_unlock_level(class_id, ruleset)`: under ruleset `2024` every class unlocks its subclass at level 3 (verified by script: all 12 XPHB class records place their Subclass feature at 3, so cleric 1, sorcerer 1, warlock 1, wizard 2 and druid 2 become 3); `2014`, `mixed` and unknown rulesets keep the old `SUBCLASS_UNLOCK_LEVEL` table (U9: `mixed` stays on the SRD chassis). Used by `resolve()` (a subclass below the level is ignored with a `pruned` entry "unlocks at <class> level 3 ..."; it reads the raw `draft["ruleset"]` because the class loop runs before ruleset validation, and only the exact string "2024" matters) and by `/api/catalog/class/<id>?ruleset=` (`subclassUnlockLevel`). No frontend change was needed: ClassPage already refetches the class detail with `ruleset` whenever the draft ruleset changes and gates the subclass select on `subclassUnlockLevel`. Tests: `tests/test_subclass_unlock.py` (10 new). Server suite 1232.
- **O3 2024 spell counts and half-caster round-up (done, ruleset "2024" only, U9).** `official/subclasses.load_class_2024_progressions(class_dir)` reads the XPHB class records from the same class files as the subclasses and `Catalog.class_2024_progressions[class]` = `{"caster": full|artificer|pact, "cantrips": [20] or [], "prepared": [20]}` for the 8 casters (bard, cleric, druid, paladin, ranger, sorcerer, warlock, wizard). `_effective_caster(catalog, rc, ruleset)` returns, under 2024, a COPY of the class's spellcasting dict with `cantrips_known` from `cantripProgression`, `spells_known` = `preparedSpellsProgression[level-1]` (fixed, no ability modifier) and `spells_kind: "prepared"` (so every 2024 caster's card is labelled "Prepared Spells"), plus slots from `single_class_spell_slots(..., ruleset)`. `engine/spells.py`: under 2024 `single_class_spell_slots` gives Paladin/Ranger the full-caster row at caster level `ceil(level/2)` (slots from level 1; checked against the XPHB Paladin table for all 20 levels) and `combined_caster_level(entries, catalog, ruleset)` / `spell_slots_for(catalog, entries, ruleset)` round half casters up. `2014`, `mixed` and unknown rulesets are untouched (regression tests). Verified XPHB values (hand-read from the tables, asserted in `tests/test_class_2024_spells.py`): Wizard 1/5/20 cantrips 3/4/5, prepared 4/9/25; Cleric 5 prepared 9; Sorcerer 1 cantrips 4, prepared 2; Bard 10 cantrips 4, prepared 15; Druid 3 prepared 6; Warlock 1 cantrips 2 / prepared 2; Paladin 1/2/5/9/20 prepared 2/3/6/9/15 and slots `{1:2}`, `{1:2}`, `{1:4,2:2}`, `{1:4,2:3,3:2}`, `{1:4,2:3,3:3,4:3,5:2}` at level 20; Ranger 1 and 9 prepared 2 and 9; Paladin 3 + Wizard 3 = caster level 5 (`{1:4,2:3,3:2}`) under 2024 vs level 4 (`{1:4,2:3}`) otherwise. No new sheet or derived keys, so `export_fields.py` and `types.ts` are unchanged. The 2024 Wizard spellbook table was modelled later in C4; the Warlock/other pact tables are still the SRD ones (identical to XPHB for slots). Server suite 1263.

## Class resources, 2024 chassis and small gaps - S1 (2026-10-09)
- **S1 spell slots in the frontend (done, frontend only).** New `frontend/src/components/SpellSlotsSummary.tsx` renders plain `<p>` lines from `resolution.derived.spellSlots` (shared pool, keys are level strings, e.g. `{"1": 4, "2": 3}`) and `derived.warlockSpellSlots` (Pact Magic, a single level, e.g. `{"3": 2}`): "Клетки за заклинания: ниво 1 ×4, ниво 2 ×3" and "Магия на договора: 2 × ниво 3". Used at the top of the ClassPage spells tab panel (per class block, `classId` passed) and on ExportPage (no `classId`, both lines). The pact line shows only on the Warlock block; the shared pool shows on every block of a class that has a `sheet.spells` entry. The note "(общи за всички класове)" is added when more than one non-Warlock class has a `sheet.spells` entry. Disclosed approximation: `sheet.spells` has an entry only for classes with a spell choice or always-prepared spells, which is a proxy for "grants slots" (a Paladin/Ranger with an entry but no slots yet is not distinguished). No server, `types.ts` or CSS change.
- Browser check at 375px (fresh drafts, mixed ruleset): Wizard 5 "ниво 1 ×4, ниво 2 ×3, ниво 3 ×2"; Warlock 5 "2 × ниво 3"; Eldritch Knight 3 "ниво 1 ×2"; Wizard 3 / Warlock 2 Wizard block "ниво 1 ×4, ниво 2 ×2" (no shared note), Warlock block shared line plus "2 × ниво 1"; ExportPage shows both lines. `scrollWidth` 375 in all. Frontend build, server suite 1263, root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped.

## Class resources, 2024 chassis and small gaps - S2 (2026-10-09)
- **S2 cross-edition same-name feat duplicates (done, decision D4 = yes under every ruleset).** A feat counts as already taken when another held feat has the same name key (`_feat_name_key`: lowercased name, trailing " (...)" stripped), unless the feat being picked is `repeatable`. The "taken" collections in `resolve.py` changed from index sets to dicts built by `_taken_add` (`{index: ""}` plus `{"name:<key>": "<Name>, <SOURCE>"}`) and are queried through `_taken_reason(feat, taken)`; used by `_asi_choice_templates` (per-level `taken_by_level`), `_resolve_asi_selected_feats`, and `_species_feat_choice_template`. Background grants, species grants and earlier ASI picks all feed the same map (first holder wins, order background, species, ASI by class order and level). Disabled reason: unchanged `already taken` for the same catalog feat, `already taken (Alert, XPHB)` for a same-name feat of another edition; `_resolve_species_selected_feats` matches by prefix. Problem id unchanged: `feat-duplicate-<index>-<cid>`.
- **Verified by script (real catalog).** 47 name keys occur more than once. XPHB-style backgrounds granting Alert: criminal-xphb, guard-xphb, ice-fisher-frhof, inquisitive-efa (all `alert-xphb`). Only three name pairs involve `repeatable`: Elemental Adept, Magic Initiate, Skilled, and in each the XPHB version is `repeatable` and the PHB version is not. Consequence (RAW-consistent, the rule keys on the feat being picked): PHB first then XPHB is allowed; XPHB first then PHB is blocked; XPHB after XPHB is allowed. Under `2014` and `2024` the pool holds one edition, so nothing changes (A1 counts 123/129 + 21 combos unchanged).
- Tests: `tests/test_feat_cross_edition_duplicates.py` (8).
- Server suite 1271 passed (1263 + 8), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped. No frontend change.

## Class resources, 2024 chassis and small gaps - S3 (2026-10-09)
- **S3 Four Elements grants Elemental Attunement automatically (done).** Verified by script over the real class files: only Way of the Four Elements PHB carries `optionalfeatureProgression[].required` (`{"3": ["Elemental Attunement|PHB"]}`, progression 3:2, 6:3, 11:4, 17:5); the option id is `opt-elemental-attunement-phb` (the `convert_optional_features` slug rule). `convert_subclasses` carries it as `feature_specific.optional_progression.required = {"3": ["opt-elemental-attunement-phb"]}` (`_required_option_ids`; unknown names are dropped; the key is absent for Battle Master, Arcane Archer, Rune Knight, Swords).
- `resolve._optional_progression_template`: required ids at reached levels leave `options`, `count` drops by their number (level 3: 1 pick of 16 options, level 6: 2), the template gets `granted: [{id, label}]` and its description appends "Elemental Attunement is granted automatically.". The generic choice pass drops a stored granted id from the raw selection before resolving, so an old draft with [attunement, fangs] resolves to [fangs] with no `pruned` entry and no over-selected problem. `sheet_features._class_choice_labels` lists granted labels first (the sheet feature "Way of the Four Elements" shows `choice: ["Elemental Attunement", ...]`, even before any pick), and `_class_feature_list` includes the granted id. No frontend or `types.ts` change (`granted` is an extra key on the choice object; the description carries the text).
- Tests: `tests/test_four_elements_attunement.py` (9); `test_subclass_import.py::test_rune_knight_and_four_elements_...` changed on purpose (Four Elements level 3: 16 options, count 1, was 17 and 2). Server suite 1280 passed, root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped.

## Class resources, 2024 chassis and small gaps - S4 (2026-10-09)
- **S4 build-time option choices from feature text (done, decision D10 = build-time only).** `reference_data.FEATURE_TEXT_CHOICES` (feature id -> pick count) lists exactly four imported ids, each verified by script against the real class files: `totem-warrior-phb-3-totem-spirit`, `totem-warrior-phb-6-aspect-of-the-beast`, `totem-warrior-phb-14-totemic-attunement` (count 1 each) and `storm-herald-xge-3-storm-aura` (the raw block has no `count`, default 1). Scan of the 27 `options`-block features: the rest are optional-feature refs (maneuvers, shots, runes, disciplines, Swords fighting style), grant-all (Psionic Power, Soul Blades, Telekinetic Adept, Genie's Vessel, Tipsy Sway, Kensei), per-rest (The Third Eye, Master Transmuter), follow-the-first-pick (Storm Soul, Raging Storm: derived text only, no card), or SRD Hunter features (already `subfeature_options`). None of those were added.
- **Converter** (`official/subclasses.py::convert_subclasses`, `_text_options`): for an allowlisted feature, the first top-level `options` block becomes `feature_specific.text_options = {"count", "options": [{"id", "name", "desc"}]}` from its `refSubclassFeature` children; the text is still inlined into `desc`. Option id = `<featureId>--<slug(option name)>`, e.g. `totem-warrior-phb-3-totem-spirit--bear` (slice R5 keys the Bear resistance off it).
- **Resolve**: `_feature_choice_templates` has a `text_options` branch (`_text_options_template`), cid `class:<cls>:<lvl>:feature:<featureId>`, so `sheet_features` lists the pick (sheet feature "Totem Spirit" has choice ["Bear"]) and the generic pass prunes picks when the level drops (6 -> 5 prunes the Aspect pick; the Totem Spirit pick stays). Option objects are `{id, label, description}` with no name prefix in the description. No frontend change (generic choice card).
- **Option counts (real data).** Totem Spirit, Aspect of the Beast and Totemic Attunement each have 5 options: Bear, Eagle, Elk, Tiger, Wolf. The plan expected Elk/Tiger as separate SCAG entries; in the data they are SCAG-sourced children inside the SAME options block, so they are in the same card. They are not hidden when SCAG is excluded in `draft.sources` (disclosed; the optional-progression pools are not source-filtered either). Storm Aura: Desert, Sea, Tundra.
- Tests: `tests/test_feature_text_choices.py` (12). Server suite 1292 passed, root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped.

## Class resources, 2024 chassis and small gaps - S5 (2026-10-09)
- **S5 Dueling as a separate field (done, decision D3 = b).** `engine/attacks.weapon_attack` no longer bakes the Dueling +2 into `damage`. New `duelingDamage` (one-handed dice + mod + 2) is present only for melee, non-two-handed weapons when `styles["one_handed_melee_damage"]` is set (Dueling fighting style, Swords bard Dueling, etc.); otherwise `null`. The note "Dueling: only with no other weapon" is kept. There is still no hands model, so the "no other weapon" check stays a note.
- **Versatile weapons:** they get `duelingDamage` (Dueling applies to the one-handed grip: longsword `damage "1d8+3"`, `duelingDamage "1d8+5"`); `versatileDamage` (two-handed grip) never gets the bonus, as before. Two-handed and ranged weapons get none.
- **Behavior change:** `damage` for a Dueling character is now the plain value; tests that asserted Dueling inside `damage` (`test_engine_attacks.py`, the Swords bard test in `test_subclass_import.py`) were updated in the same commit.
- Exports/UI: `Attack.duelingDamage` in `types.ts`; Markdown/PDF "Атаки" bullet adds ", с Дуел <dmg>" after the versatile text; ExportPage adds ", with Dueling <dmg>" (plain text, no CSS). YAML carries the field automatically via `derived.attacks`.
- Tests: server 1295 passed (+3), root 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend build green.


## Class resources, 2024 chassis and small gaps - R1 (2026-10-09)
- **R1 resource framework, Barbarian and Rogue (done, decisions D1 = `derived.classResources`, D2 = mixed uses SRD numbers).** `derived.classResources` is a flat list in class order (a class's subclass entries follow its own). Shape: `{id "<class>:<name-slug>", classId, source {kind class|subclass, id, label}, name, uses, unlimited? (only when true; uses is then null), recharge long|short|short-or-long|null, die?, dice?, pool?, bonus?, dc?, details?}`; optional fields that are None are omitted. Barbarian gives `Rage` (`uses`, `recharge "long"`, `details {damageBonus, resistances [bludgeoning, piercing, slashing]}`); Rogue gives `Sneak Attack` (`dice "NdF"`, `uses` null, `recharge` null).
- **Loader.** `official/subclasses.load_class_2024_tables(class_dir)` -> `{class: {column slug: [20 values]}}` from the XPHB `classTableGroups` and `load_subclass_2024_tables` -> `{subclass id: {...}}` from `subclassTableGroups` (ids use `_sub_id`, e.g. `psi-warrior-xphb`). Groups without exactly 20 `rows` (the `rowsSpellProgression` slot tables) are skipped, so are columns whose label has a `|spells|` filter, "Spell Slots" and "Slot Level". Label slug: `{@filter X|..}` / `{@tip X|..}` -> X. Values: digit strings -> int, `{type: bonus|bonusSpeed}` -> value, `{type: dice}` -> `"NdF"`, `{@dice D6}` -> `"d6"`. `Catalog.class_2024_tables` / `subclass_2024_tables` (default `{}`) are filled next to `class_2024_progressions`. Real columns: barbarian rages / rage-damage / weapon-mastery; bard bardic-die; cleric, paladin channel-divinity; druid wild-shape; fighter second-wind / weapon-mastery; monk martial-arts / focus-points / unarmored-movement; ranger favored-enemy; rogue sneak-attack; sorcerer sorcery-points; warlock invocations. Subclass tables exist only for Psi Warrior and Soulknife XPHB (`die-size`, `number`).
- **Engine.** New pure `engine/class_resources.py`: `resources_for(catalog, resolved_classes, modifiers, prof_bonus, ruleset, choices=None, warnings_out=None)`. `_BUILDERS[class_id] -> builder(ctx, rc)` (and an empty `_SUBCLASS_BUILDERS[subclass_id]` for R6). A builder reads SRD numbers from `rc["level_row"]["class_specific"]` and asks `ctx.table(class, column, level)` for the 2024 value (None unless ruleset is exactly "2024" and the column exists, in which case the builder keeps the SRD number). To add a class (R2-R6): write `_name(ctx, rc)` with the `_resource(...)` helper and register it. `resolve()` calls it once, just before the `derived` dict, with the `warnings` list.
- **Verified by script (real data).** XPHB Rage Damage by level: 2 (1-8), 3 (9-15), 4 (16-20), equal to the SRD `rage_damage_bonus` at every level. XPHB Rages equal the SRD `rage_count` at levels 1-19; the only difference is level 20 (SRD 9999 = unlimited, 2024 = 6; level 17-19 are 6 in both). Rogue 2024 Sneak Attack equals the SRD `sneak_attack` at all 20 levels (ceil(level/2) d6). Result: unlimited Rage at 20 under 2014 and mixed, 6 under 2024.
- **Degrade.** Under "2024" with an empty `class_2024_tables` the SRD numbers are used and the warning `class-2024-tables-missing` is added once (only when a class with a table-reading builder is present). Mixed and 2014 never read the tables or warn.
- **Exports/UI.** `DERIVED_FIELDS` has `classResources` (YAML carries it); `extra_blocks` adds "Ресурси на класа" with `export_fields.resource_line` ("Rage: 3 (дълга почивка), щета +2", "Sneak Attack: 2d6", unlimited as "без ограничение"; recharge words "кратка почивка", "дълга почивка", "кратка или дълга почивка"). Frontend: `ClassResource` and optional `Derived.classResources` in `types.ts`, new `components/ClassResourcesList.tsx` (plain `<ul>`, same wording), used in each ClassPage block (filtered by classId) and on ExportPage; no CSS.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, level 20, human/acolyte, mean of 20): Fighter mixed 2.68 ms before, 2.78 ms after; Barbarian mixed 2.04 -> 1.98 ms; 2024 ruleset Fighter 1.72 ms, Barbarian 1.38 ms after (limit 50 ms).
- Tests: `tests/test_class_resources.py` (60). Server suite 1355 passed (1295 + 60), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend build green.

## Class resources - R2 Fighter and Monk, attacks per action, Monk unarmed strike (2026-10-09)
- **Fighter** (`_fighter`): `Second Wind` (`recharge "short-or-long"`, `details.heal "1d10+<fighter level>"`; uses 1 under 2014/mixed, the XPHB `second-wind` table under 2024: 2 at 1-3, 3 at 4-9, 4 at 10-20), `Action Surge` (SRD `action_surges`, 1 at 2 and 2 at 17, short-or-long) and `Indomitable` (SRD `indomitable_uses`, 1/2/3 at 9/13/17, long); the last two are omitted while 0. The 2024 table has no Action Surge/Indomitable column, so the SRD rows serve every ruleset.
- **Monk** (`_monk`): `Martial Arts` (`die "d6"`), plus `Ki` (2014/mixed, `ki_points`) or `Focus Points` (2024 table `focus-points`), `recharge "short"`, `dc = 8 + PB + WIS` (resource omitted at level 1, where both are 0). Martial Arts die verified by script: 2014 d4 (1-4), d6 (5-10), d8 (11-16), d10 (17-20); 2024 d6, d8, d10, d12 at the same breakpoints (XPHB column `martial-arts`, "1d6" etc.). Focus Points 0 at level 1 then equal to level, as the plan said. `export_fields.resource_line` and `ClassResourcesList` now also print `details.heal` ("лекуване 1d10+5").
- **Attacks per action** (`class_resources.attacks_per_action`, `derived.attacksPerAction`): 1 + the largest extra-attack count of any source (PHB 164, no stacking). Fighter reads `extra_attacks` from the level row (1/2/3 at 5/11/20, the only class with that column). The other sources are the feature ids in `EXTRA_ATTACK_FEATURES` (verified: these are exactly the catalog features named "Extra Attack" besides the Fighter's `extra-attack-1/2/3`): `barbarian|monk|paladin|ranger-extra-attack` (level 5), bard `valor-phb|swords-xge|valor-xphb` and wizard `bladesinging-tce|bladesinger-frhof` (level 6, only with that subclass). Example: Fighter 5 / Barbarian 5 = 2, Fighter 11 / Monk 5 = 3, Fighter 20 = 4. Not affected by ruleset.
- **Unarmed strike** (`attacks.unarmed_strike`, id `unarmed-strike`): appended after the weapon rows by `resolve()` when Monk level >= 1; better of STR/DEX (tie STR), always proficient, `damage "<1dN>+mod"` from the Monk's Martial Arts resource die, `bludgeoning`, range "5", note "Martial Arts". Verified that no catalog item has that id or "unarmed" in its name. Known limit: the row is not changed by the Monk Weapon Mastery/Fighting Style, nor by two-weapon rules.
- **Exports/UI.** `attacksPerAction` is in `DERIVED_FIELDS`; the Markdown/PDF "Атаки" block starts with "Атаки на действие: N" (the block is also printed when there are no attack rows but N > 1). ExportPage shows "Attacks per action: N" as the first plain list item; `Derived.attacksPerAction?` in `types.ts`. No CSS; the 375px width check was not run in a browser (plain `<li>` lines only).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, level 20, human/acolyte, mean of 20): Fighter mixed 2.65 ms, 2024 1.67 ms; Monk mixed 1.93 ms, 2024 1.31 ms (limit 50 ms; R1 Fighter was 2.78 ms).
- Tests: `tests/test_class_resources_fighter_monk.py` (67, rule matrix in the docstring); the R1 test that used a Fighter as the "no builder" class now uses a Paladin. Server suite 1422 passed (1355 + 67), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend build green.

## Class resources - R3 Cleric, Paladin, Druid, Channel Divinity multiclass (2026-10-09)
- **Cleric** (`_cleric`): `Channel Divinity` (2014/mixed: SRD `channel_divinity_charges` 1/2/3 at 2/6/18, `recharge "short-or-long"`; 2024: XPHB `channel-divinity` column 2/3/4 at 2/6/18, `recharge "long"` with `details.shortRestRegains 1`, because XPHB regains one use on a short rest and all on a long rest) and, 2014/mixed only, `Destroy Undead` (`details.maxCr` "1/2","1","2","3","4" at 5/8/11/14/17 from `destroy_undead_cr`; no uses). The 2024 Cleric has Sear Undead, which has no CR column, so no entry.
- **Paladin** (`_paladin`): `Lay on Hands` (`pool = 5 x level`, long rest, both editions, verified in the SRD and XPHB text); `Divine Sense` only under 2014/mixed (`uses = max(1, 1 + CHA mod)`, long rest; in 2024 it is a Channel Divinity effect); `Channel Divinity` from level 3: 2014/mixed 1 use (hardcoded `reference_data.PALADIN_2014_CHANNEL_DIVINITY`, the SRD level rows have no column and the feature text is empty), 2024 table 2 (3-10), 3 (11-20).
- **Druid** (`_druid`): `Wild Shape` from level 2. 2014/mixed: 2 uses (hardcoded `WILD_SHAPE_2014_USES`, from the feature text), short-or-long, `details {maxCr, swim, fly}` from `wild_shape_max_cr/swim/fly`; `unlimited` at level 20 (SRD feature `archdruid`). Verified SRD rows: CR 1/4 (2-3), 1/2 with swim (4-6), 1 with swim (7), 1 with swim and fly (8+); note the SRD row already gives CR 1 at level 7 (the feature text only changes at 8, where flying is added), so the plan's "1 at 8" is the fly step. 2024: XPHB `wild-shape` column 2 (2-5), 3 (6-16), 4 (17-20), not unlimited at 20 (XPHB Archdruid regains a use on initiative); `details {maxCr, fly, knownForms, shortRestRegains 1}` from the prose-only Beast Shapes table, hardcoded as `WILD_SHAPE_2024_BEAST_SHAPES` (CR 1/4 no fly at 2, 1/2 at 4, 1 with fly at 8; 4/6/8 known forms; no swim restriction in 2024). Level 1 has no entry.
- **Channel Divinity multiclass** (PHB 164, "...getting the feature again doesn't give you an additional use of it"): `resources_for` ends with `_merge_channel_divinity`; one entry, `uses` = max over classes, `classId` = the class giving the max (first class on a tie), `details.classes` lists every class that grants it (only when more than one). Cleric 6 / Paladin 3 = one entry, 2 uses (cleric); 2024 Cleric 6 / Paladin 3 = 3, not 5. Applied under every ruleset. Open question: the XPHB data has no multiclassing rules text, so the 2024 multiclass sentence could not be verified; the XPHB class text says "this class's Channel Divinity" (separate pools?). Needs a user decision (see the report).
- **Exports/UI.** `resource_line` and `describeResource` now also print `details.maxCr` ("макс. CR 1/2"); `maxCr?` in `types.ts`. Otherwise unchanged. Verified-by-script facts and the multiclass case matrix are in the test docstring.
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20): Cleric 20 mixed 2.55 ms, 2024 1.92 ms; Cleric 6 / Paladin 3 mixed 1.29 ms, 2024 1.15 ms (limit 50 ms).
- Tests: `tests/test_class_resources_cleric_paladin_druid.py` (92); the R1 "no builder" test now uses a Ranger. Server suite 1514 passed (1422 + 92), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped, frontend build green.

## Class resources - R4 Bard, Sorcerer, Wizard, 2024 Ranger (2026-10-09)
- **Bard** (`_bard`): `Bardic Inspiration`, `die` d6/d8/d10/d12 at 1/5/10/15 (2024: XPHB `bardic-die` column, checked level by level against SRD `bardic_inspiration_die`: identical; 2014/mixed: the SRD row), `uses = max(1, CHA mod)` in both editions, `recharge` long below level 5 and `short-or-long` from 5 (Font of Inspiration; same text in SRD and XPHB). 2024 adds `details.spellSlotRegainsUse` from level 5 (XPHB Font of Inspiration: spend a slot to regain one use). Without the 2024 tables the SRD die is used.
- **Sorcerer** (`_sorcerer`): `Sorcery Points` from level 2 (uses = level, long rest; SRD `sorcery_points` / XPHB column, identical). `details.slotCosts` = {slot level: cost}. 2014/mixed: SRD `creating_spell_slots` (1-5 = 2/3/5/6/7, all from level 2, the 2014 text has no minimum level). 2024: same costs but only slots whose minimum sorcerer level (2/3/5/7/9, prose table in the XPHB Font of Magic text) is reached; hardcoded as `reference_data.SORCERER_2024_CREATING_SLOTS`. Dict keys are ints in the resolved dict (strings after JSON). Not added (not in the R4 plan): Innate Sorcery (2024, 2 uses) and Sorcerous Restoration.
- **Wizard** (`_wizard`): `Arcane Recovery`, `pool = ceil(level/2)`, `uses None`, long rest, `details {maxSlotLevel 5, onShortRest}`.
