Martial Adept: feat resource "Superiority Die (Martial Adept)" (1 d6, short-or-long, DC 8 + PB + better of STR / DEX, note lists the maneuvers), separate from the Battle Master dice. Metamagic Adept: feat resource "Sorcery Points (Metamagic Adept)" (2, long rest, Metamagic only), separate from the Sorcerer's pool. `{choice}` in a note is the picked labels (`feat_effects._picks`).
- **Rules.** `resolve._gate_feat_invocation_prereqs`: an invocation with any prerequisite is open only to a Warlock who meets it (level, Pact Boon or a 2024 Pact invocation, Eldritch Blast); a non-Warlock has level 0, so only the 11 prerequisite-free ones are open; a stored pick that fails is dropped with a `pruned` entry. `resolve._gate_duplicate_picks`: one option cannot be held twice across the families fighting styles (SRD class cards, 2024 Fighting Style feat cards, Fighting Initiate), maneuvers (Battle Master, Martial Adept), Metamagic (Sorcerer SRD / 2024, Metamagic Adept) and invocations (Warlock, Eldritch Adept). Cards are walked in build order (origin feats, then (class index, level), a class card before the ASI of its level); the later holder is the duplicate (option disabled "already taken (Dueling, SRD)", a stored duplicate dropped with a `pruned` entry); options compare by name, so the 2014 and 2024 editions of one option are the same. The walk runs only for families in which a feat card takes part, and a class card is checked only against feat holders, so a character without these feats is byte-identical (the golden would otherwise have caught Sorcerer drafts that repeat a Metamagic option across their own cards).
- **Limits.** The TCE-only fighting styles (Blind Fighting, Interception, Thrown, Unarmed, Superior Technique), Seeking / Transmuted Spell and any XPHB invocation are not in the pools (not imported; Eldritch Adept offers the SRD list under every ruleset). A 2024 Fighting Style feat card is only a holder: picked after a Fighting Initiate style it does not see it (the feat is already resolved). Eldritch Adept's Agonizing Blast / Thirsting Blade style notes for a Warlock come through the generic class resource entry (no per-instance target cantrip card). Dual-source position uses the (class index, level) convention of F1.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_feat_option_picks.py` (51 tests, rule matrix in its docstring); with the `resolve.py` / `features.py` / `feat_effects.py` / `class_resources.py` wiring reverted 39 fail. Edited expectations: `test_feat_resources` (FE9 entries excluded from the FE3 text list; resource keys `die` / `dc`; 12 resources), `test_feat_effects_coverage` ceiling.
- **Totals.** Server 3459 -> 3510 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped (frontend untouched, no build). Timing `resolve()` (mean of 10, ms): Wizard 20 mixed with all four feats 4.0, Wizard 20 2024 with all four 3.4, Fighter 20 mixed 2.9, Fighter 20 2024 1.9, Wizard 20 mixed 3.0 (< 50 ms).

## FE10 Feat effects: resource and note tail, `FEAT_PENDING` emptied (2026-10-12)

Eleventh slice of the FE series (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-feat-effects.md`). Every one of the 305 catalog feats now has an entry in `FEAT_EFFECTS`; `FEAT_PENDING` is empty, `PENDING_CEILING = 0`, `FE10_DONE = True`, and the ratchet test asserts `FEAT_PENDING == frozenset()` and `set(FEAT_EFFECTS) == set(catalog.feats)`. Each feat follows its own edition's text under every ruleset (keyed by feat index; kept-but-hidden picks keep their entries). Decision F-D8 applied (FE11 skipped): the eight Arcane Adept feats of Unearthed Arcana (slot-keyed spell tables), Potent Dragonmark's extra slot, Rune Shaper, Boon of Magic School Mastery and Magic Connoisseur are notes only. The F0 golden holds 0 feats and stayed green without regeneration (`STRIP` unchanged). No new output keys, so `types.ts`, the exports and the frontend are untouched (no build needed): the notes and resources use the FE3 surfaces (`sheet.feats[].notes`, `derived.featResources`, `die` from FE9).

- **Data.** New `server/catalog/feat_effects_tail.py` (pure data; `feat_effects_data` merges `TAIL_EFFECTS` / `TAIL_QUOTES` at its end). 170 feats classified: 67 with resources (74 resources, `uses` an int, `"pb"` or the new `"inc_mod"`; recharge long, short-or-long), 9 `data_only` (everything is a catalog field: Drow High Magic, Fey Teleportation, Fey Touched (TCE), Shadow Touched (TCE), Initiate of High Sorcery, Outlands Envoy, Strixhaven Initiate, Svirfneblin Magic, Wood Elf Magic; a test checks each carries spell or language fields) and 94 notes only (spell grants are listed with the non-spell benefits; tables are summarised). Notes paraphrase the catalog text with the character's numbers (DCs such as `{8+pb+inc_mod}` and `{13+pb}`, Temporary Hit Points, `{2*level}`, `{pb//2}` runes, Potent Dragonmark's `{min(5, (level+1)//2)}` slot level, Chef's `{4+pb}`); the governing sentence(s) of every entry are pinned in `TAIL_QUOTES` against the catalog text.
- **Completion of earlier entries.** `COMPLETION` appends the text FE1-FE6 left out of 13 entries they had classified only for their number: Alert (both editions), Athlete (XPHB), Boon of Fortitude (the extra Constitution-modifier healing), Boon of Speed (Escape Artist), Keen Mind and Observant (XPHB bonus actions), Keenness of the Stone Giant (Stone Throw resource, PB uses, long rest), Mark of Passage, Mobile, Speedy, Squat Nimbleness and Transmuted Anatomy (Resilient Anatomy resource). The first quote of each stays in `FEAT_EFFECT_QUOTES` (a string, as FE1 tests expect); the added text is pinned in `FEAT_EFFECT_EXTRA_QUOTES`.
- **Engine.** `engine/feat_effects.resources`: `uses: "inc_mod"` (Spell Subterfuge: uses equal to the modifier of the ability the feat increased; a negative modifier gives 0; before the ability is chosen the resource shows 0 uses and its note ends with "(choose the ability)"). Nothing else in the engine changed.
- **Already carried by catalog fields (verified, no entry needed beyond a note).** Resistances, immunities, condition immunities, senses, Expertise, languages and spells reach the sheet for Boon of Terror (Frightened immunity, Intimidation Expertise), Boon of Truesight, Aberrant Anatomy (Blindsight 15, Perception Expertise), Infernal Constitution, Boon of Poison Mastery, Boon of Blazing Dawn, Soul Drinker, Furious Storm, Ember of the Fire Giant, Fury of the Frost Giant, Mark of Storm, Cult of the Dragon Initiate (Draconic) and Fey Teleportation (Sylvan); a test pins each. Conditional resistances (Soul of the Storm Giant's aura, Boon of Desperate Resilience while Bloodied, Boon of the Night Spirit) are notes, not permanent resistances.
- **Choices that last one rest (F-D7).** Planar Adaptation (Planar Wanderer) and Versatile Merc (Zhentarim Tactics) are notes only.
- **Limits.** Cult of the Dragon Initiate: if you already know Draconic you learn another language; the app lists Draconic only, the note says to add the replacement by hand. Ability Score Improvement (XPHB) as a feat is excluded from every ASI pool by design (the class level offers the ability combos), so its entry is a note. The Arcane Adept tables are listed in the note, not resolved against the character's highest spell slot. Several of the 23 feats that no ASI pool offers (Dragonmark, Dark Gift and Fighting Style Paladin / Ranger feats) are reached through the same patched-catalog pattern as FE7 in the matrix test; their grant path in the app is unchanged.
- **Tests.** `tests/test_feat_tail.py` (399 tests, rule matrix in its docstring): every feat classified, every scan-kind `resource` feat has a resource (two documented spell-table exceptions), quote pins for every tail entry and for all entries together, vocabulary guard, a parametrized resolve over all 69 resource-bearing feats at Proficiency Bonus 2 (Fighter 4) and 4 (Fighter 9), the `inc_mod` rule, a numbers matrix for notes (DCs, Temporary Hit Points, level, Proficiency Bonus), catalog-carried effects reaching the sheet, Markdown / PDF / YAML surfaces, no-feat identity, empty catalog and timing; with the data merge removed the module cannot be imported, with the `inc_mod` engine change reverted 3 tests fail. Edited expectations: `test_feat_effects_coverage` (`PENDING_CEILING` 0, empty-set assertion, every feat has an entry), `test_feat_resources` and `test_feat_effects_numeric` (the FE3 / FE1 data checks exclude the FE10 additions to their entries).
- **Totals.** Server 3510 -> 3910 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped. Timing `resolve()` (mean of 20, ms): Fighter 20 mixed with five resource feats 5.6, Fighter 20 2024 with five 3.7, Wizard 20 mixed with five 5.8, Wizard 20 2024 with five 5.0 (< 50 ms).

**Counts (305 catalog feats, recomputed in FX from `FEAT_EFFECTS`; the FE12 figures 119 / 33 / 153 mixed the categories and are superseded).** Method: an entry is `data_only` when it says so (33: everything it does is already a catalog field: 7 Fighting Style feats, 15 FE3 proficiency / save / spell feats, 9 FE10 spell / language feats, others); otherwise it is *modelled* when it has any key besides `notes` (`resources`, `attack_notes`, a number, a choice, an attack flag; `modifies` alone does not count); the rest are *notes only*. Result: **142 modelled** (60 with a numeric, choice or attack effect only, 74 with resources only, 8 with both; `attack_notes` feats count as modelled: 28 feats, 23 of them with nothing else), **33 `data_only`**, **130 notes only**; 142 + 33 + 130 = 305. Resources: 82 feats (Potent Dragonmark joined in FX6); numeric / choice / attack: 68 feats. 13 entries carry `modifies` (FX3). `FEAT_PENDING` is empty. Suite growth: server 2979 -> 3945 (FE12) -> 3984 (FX), root 405 / 8 skipped.

## FE12 Feat effects: consolidation (2026-10-12)

The F0 golden changed only additively (below). No new output keys; `types.ts` and the frontend are untouched.

- **F-D9 (2024 only; refined in FX4).** XPHB Jack of All Trades: "any ability check you make that uses a skill proficiency you lack and that doesn't otherwise use your Proficiency Bonus" (catalog `xphb-bard-2-jack-of-all-trades`). Under ruleset `2024` `resolve()` adds no Jack of All Trades half bonus to Initiative (the half bonus still applies to non-proficient skill totals and the passives built on them: skill checks only). **Remarkable Athlete is left as it was in every ruleset.** The FE12 version also dropped it under 2024, which was wrong: the XPHB Champion's Remarkable Athlete (`champion-xphb-3-remarkable-athlete`: "you have Advantage on Initiative rolls and Strength (Athletics) checks") never had the half bonus and is not mapped, while the SRD / 2014 `champion` subclass's own `remarkable-athlete` effect (half PB rounded up on STR / DEX / CON checks and Initiative) is that record's own text and now stays. Mixed and 2014 are unchanged. Alert XPHB still replaces the half bonus (FE1). Implemented in `resolve()` with `initiative_jack`; tests `tests/test_half_bonus_2024.py` (8).
- **Crossbow Expert PHB.** Official Sage Advice: the ability modifier is added to the damage of the hand crossbow bonus attack (the "no modifier unless negative" rule is two-weapon fighting's). New flag `offhand_hand_crossbow_mod` (entry `offhand.hand_crossbow_mod`); the hand crossbow row gets `offhandDamage` with the modifier (negative modifiers included), and the row note says so. Tests in `test_feat_attacks.py`; the FE5 limit above is superseded.
- **Kitchen sink.** Four feat-holding drafts in `kitchen_sink.py`: `feats-mixed-fighter14` (Alert, Tough, Great Weapon Master, Lucky, Observant, PHB), `feats-2014-rogue16` (Keen Mind, Weapon Master, Resilient, Elemental Adept x2), `feats-mixed-wizard8` (Martial Adept, Eldritch Adept), `feats-2024-fighter19` (Alert from Criminal, Tough, Great Weapon Master, Lucky, Observant, Keen Mind, Weapon Master, Boon of Fortitude). `test_export_fields._EXPECT` pins feats, resources, initiative, notes and attack notes; `_expected_strings` now also requires every attack-row note and off-hand damage in Markdown and PDF (YAML is checked by the existing key / equality tests).
- **Types drift.** `tests/test_feat_consolidation.py` resolves a Fighter 19 holding each of the 305 feats (patched-catalog route for the feats no ASI pool offers) with weapons on the sheet and runs the `test_types_drift` check on every container; `UNTYPED_ALLOWLIST` stays empty.
- **Golden (decision: add feat cases).** `test_c_series_golden.py --append` (new mode) added exactly three cases and asserted the 228 existing ones unchanged (stored filled draft and stripped result equal a fresh resolve; every entry written back identical): `ks/src-all/feats-mixed-fighter14`, `ks/src-all/feats-2014-rogue16`, `ks/src-all/feats-mixed-wizard8` (feat drafts run under `sources` [] only, since a `srd` filter would hide the picks). The 2024 draft is not in the golden. `GOLDEN_FEAT_CASES = 3`. A future change to a mixed / 2014 feat effect now fails these cases and needs `--append` (new cases) or a documented STRIP / regeneration decision.
- **Isolation.** `tests/test_chargen_isolation.py` allowlists the server-local test modules `test_feat_tail` and `test_types_drift`.
- **Timing** `resolve()` (mean of 20, ms, 5 resource feats, level 20): mixed Fighter 5.6, Monk 3.5, Wizard 5.6, Warlock 4.8, Bard 5.1, Sorcerer 5.2; 2024 Fighter 3.4, Monk 2.7, Wizard 5.0, Warlock 4.7, Bard 16.4, Sorcerer 4.3; Warlock 20 with 10 invocations 4.1 (all < 50 ms).
- **375 px check** (Vite dev server + Flask, drafts via `localStorage["dnd-builder-draft-v2"]`, mixed Fighter 14 with five feats and 2024 Fighter 19 with eight): `document.documentElement.scrollWidth` was 375 on ExportPage (feat bullets with notes, "Ресурси от таланти", attack notes) and ClassPage (all ASI feat cards, the level-4 picker open with 131 options); no element past the viewport outside the nav strip. No frontend change.
- **Totals.** Server 3910 -> 3945 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped; `npm run build` green.


## FX Review fixes for the feat-effects series (2026-10-16)

Batch of fixes from a read-only review of FE0-FE12. Golden `test_c_series_golden.py` untouched and green (no regeneration); mixed / 2014 output changes only where noted (feat effects of feats a character holds).

- **FX1 Performance** (`engine/resolve.py`). `_source_allowed` no longer rebuilds the lowered source set per call (`_lowered_sources`, `lru_cache` keyed on the `sources` tuple, unhashable junk falls back to the old path). `_spell_filter_options` took the spell visibility test (`_spell_visible`, ~100k calls per 2024 Bard 20 resolve) six times per resolve; `_visible_spell_ids` now computes the visible id set once per (spells dict identity and size, sources, ruleset) and the filter reads it (`keep` ids still bypass it; output identical, golden green). Timing, `resolve()` mean of 20, plain Bard 20 2024: `sources []` 5.04 ms -> 4.05 ms, `sources [PHB, XPHB, TCE, XGE]` 7.16 ms -> 3.28 ms (mixed unchanged at 2.4-3.0 ms). The FE12 figure of 16.4 ms for a feat-heavy 2024 Bard 20 came from the same two hotspots. Tests `test_fx_perf_caches.py`.
- **FX2 Poisoner (TCE) note.** The attack note said the creature "fails" the DC 14 Constitution save; the text says it "must succeed on a 14 Constitution saving throw or take 2d8 poison damage and become poisoned". Note reworded; `test_feat_attacks.py` pins it.
- **FX3 Cross-feat modifications.** New optional entry field `modifies` (`FEAT_EFFECT_KEYS`): `{target feat index: {"replace": [[old, new]], "recharge": {resource name: recharge}}}`, applied by `feat_effects.apply_modifies` only while the target feat is also held (notes and resources both go through it). Greater Aberrant Mark (EFA) patches Aberrant Fortitude to 1d6 and "Short Rest or Long Rest" (catalog: "you can roll 1d6 instead of 1d4. You also now regain your use of Aberrant Fortitude whenever you finish a Short Rest or Long Rest"); each of the 12 Greater Marks (EFA) turns the base mark's Intuition note from 1d4 to 1d6 ("you can roll 1d6 instead of 1d4"). The ERLW Aberrant Dragonmark is a different feat and is not patched. The remaining cross-feat texts are listed as open question 21. Tests `test_feat_modifies.py`.
- **FX4 F-D9 refinement.** See the corrected bullet in FE12: only the 2024 Jack of All Trades Initiative bonus is dropped; Remarkable Athlete is untouched in every ruleset.
- **FX5 Frontend keys.** `ExportPage.tsx` feat list used `key={f.id}`, a duplicate for repeatable feats (Elemental Adept, Magic Initiate, Skilled). Key is now `id:origin:level:index`. No other list in `frontend/src` is keyed by a feat id (`ClassResourcesList` keys resources by their instance-scoped `id`, unique). `npm run build` green.
- **FX6 Potent Dragonmark.** The extra spell slot is now the feat resource "Dragonmark Spell Slot" (1 use, Short or Long Rest), its note computes the slot level. Catalog text: "The spell slot's level is half your level (round up), to a maximum of level 5. You regain the expended slot when you finish a Short Rest or Long Rest." Level = total character level, `min(5, (level+1)//2)`; the spell slot table is not changed. Feat resources 81 -> 82 (the overview counts are recomputed above).
- **FX7 Silent clamp.** A fixed +1 (Keen Mind PHB at INT 20, Actor XPHB at CHA 20, via an ASI slot or an origin feat) lost to the cap is now reported as a non-blocking `pruned` entry ("<Feat>: INT is already at maximum (20) and the increase was ignored"; `choiceId` = the ASI card id, or `feat:<index>` / the species origin for an origin feat). The score and the problems list are unchanged.
- **FX8 Docs.** FE overview counts recomputed and the method stated; the G1-2 Elemental Adept line marked "Superseded (FE7)"; the cap-20 / uncapped background-feat statements marked "Superseded (FE2)"; F-D9 corrected; open questions 18-22 added.


## RP1 (X1) 2024 background ability increases capped at 20 (2026-10-22)

Golden `test_c_series_golden.py` untouched and green (not regenerated). Output changes only under ruleset `2024`; mixed and 2014 are unchanged.

- **Rule.** PHB 2024: "None of these increases can raise a score above 20." In `engine/resolve.py`, right after the background choice bundle is applied (species increases are off under 2024, so the step sits on the base scores), each ability's background delta is clamped with `asi.clamped_delta(..., ASI_CAP)`. The lost part is a non-blocking `pruned` entry `{choiceId: background:<id>:ability-bonus, reason: "<Background>: STR is already at maximum (20) and the increase was ignored."}`, appended before the FX7 notes. It is never a problem.
- **Order.** The clamp runs before origin feats (FE2 clamp), species feats and ASI progression, which therefore read the clamped score and keep their own caps (20, or 30 for Epic Boons). The background card's option list is not disabled (a bundle such as `str-2+dex-1` stays selectable; only the over-cap part is dropped).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_background_cap_2024.py` (8): 20+2, 19+2, 18+2, +1/+1/+1 with one at 20, two scores over, a later-book background (carouser-abh), mixed / 2014 guards, ASI after the clamp. Seven of them fail without the fix.
- **Timing** `resolve()` (mean of 10, ms, level 20): mixed Fighter 4.0, Wizard 3.7, Bard 3.7; 2024 Fighter 2.6, Wizard 2.8, Bard 3.9.
- **Totals.** Server 3984 -> 3992 passed; root 405 passed, 8 skipped; frontend untouched.

## RP2 (B2) 2024 fixed numeric effects of XPHB class and subclass features (2026-10-22)

- **Table.** `reference_data.CLASS_FEATURE_EFFECTS_2024`, keyed by the imported XPHB feature record id. Each entry carries `quotes` (verbatim sentences of the record, pinned by `test_quotes_match_catalog`) plus the `CLASS_FEATURE_EFFECTS` keys and new ones: `supersedes`, `senses_additive`, `sense_range`, `climb_equals_walk`, `swim_equals_walk`, `speed_bonus_flat`, `resist`, `skills`, `proficiencies`. `CLASS_FEATURE_NOTES_ONLY_2024` lists the conditional or per-rest-choice features that are deliberately not effects (Stride of the Elements, Power of the Wilds, Aspect of the Wilds, Stormborn, Full of Stars, Rage of the Gods, Aura of Warding, Nature's Ward, Fiendish Resilience, Elemental Epitome), each with the conditional wording pinned.
- **Collector.** `features.xphb_effects_2024(catalog, resolved_classes, ruleset)`: ruleset "2024" only; applies an entry when the record is in the catalog, the class level has reached the record's own `level` and, for a subclass feature, the record's subclass is the selected one. `features.class_effects` merges them with the SRD effects; an entry with `supersedes` drops that SRD id only when the XPHB record was found (so an SRD-only catalog keeps the SRD Primal Champion, cap 24). The merged list feeds the existing readers (ability bonuses, AC formulas, HP per level, Fast Movement key, `swim_equals_walk`).
- **Entries.** Body and Mind (DEX/WIS +4, cap 25), Primal Champion (cap 25, replaces the SRD 24), Draconic Resilience XPHB (HP = Sorcerer level, AC 10 + DEX + CHA, shield allowed: the text says "armor", not "armor or a Shield"), Dance Unarmored Defense (10 + DEX + CHA), Shadow and Gloom Stalker Darkvision (60, or +60 on the final range, added after every other source), Thief climb, Sea swim, Glory 7 Speed +10, War 17 b/p/s, psychic (Psi Warrior 10, Aberrant 6, Great Old One 10), Celestial 6 radiant, Mercy Insight + Medicine + Herbalism Kit. Added beyond the plan's list, same kind (unconditional, fixed numbers): Ranger 6 Roving (+10 unless Heavy armor, climb and swim = Speed) and Ranger 18 Feral Senses (Blindsight 30).
- **Cap note.** An XPHB ability increase cut by its cap is a non-blocking `pruned` entry, `choiceId` `feature:<record id>` ("Body and Mind: DEX is already at maximum (25) and the increase was ignored." or "... capped at 25; only +N of +M applied."). The SRD Primal Champion under mixed / 2014 reports nothing, as before.
- **Design note (Dance).** The catalog text of Dance Unarmored Defense states no armor or shield condition at all. Pinned to the text: shield allowed; body armor replaces the formula through `armor_class_from_gear` as for every formula. Mercy's skills are added to the proficiency set (no replacement card when a class pick already took Insight or Medicine; subclass proficiency picks are RP12).
- **Not changed.** No new sheet / derived keys (`types.ts`, exports untouched; frontend untouched). Mixed and 2014 are byte-identical (F0 golden green, not regenerated).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20): Fighter 20 2.17 ms (2024) / 3.32 ms (mixed), Wizard 20 2.72 / 3.91, Bard 20 3.83 / 3.23, Monk 20 Shadow 1.76, Sorcerer 6 Draconic XPHB 1.45, Barbarian 20 1.75 (limit 50 ms).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_xphb_feature_effects_2024.py` (37; 24 behaviour tests failed before the wiring). Server suite 3992 -> 4029 passed (F0 golden green, not regenerated), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched.

## RP3 (A1, open question 13) Cult of the Dragon Initiate replacement language (2026-10-22)

Golden `test_c_series_golden.py` untouched and green (not regenerated; no golden case holds the feat). Feat completion, so it follows the FE edition policy: the feat's own text under every ruleset in which the character holds it (mixed, 2014 and 2024 alike).

- **Rule** (catalog `entries`, Dragon's Tongue): "If you already know Draconic when you select this feat, you instead learn one language of your choice from the language tables in the Player's Handbook or Chapter 2 of this book." Table `reference_data.FEAT_LANGUAGE_REPLACEMENT` (feat index -> language, label, quote); the quote is pinned against the catalog text. No FRHoF-specific language table exists in the catalog, so the pool is the PHB 2024 Standard + Rare tables (E2, 19 options), documented here.
- **Card.** `class_choices_2024.feat_replacement_language_template` builds `<feat cid prefix>:languages` (count 1; label "Cult of the Dragon Initiate: Dragon's Tongue") for every holder, from `_feat_choice_templates` (all five call sites). The `:languages` suffix means `chosen_languages` and the sheet language list pick the pick up unchanged. The fixed Draconic still shows.
- **Condition (gate).** `gate_languages` keeps the card only when Draconic is already known at the feat's position, not counting the feat's own grant (removed once from the fixed list or the positioned list). Origin feats (background, species): fixed species / background languages and picks of other species / background / feat language cards; the C8 `origin:languages` card does not count (its Draconic is disabled by the feat's fixed grant, C8). Lessons of the First Ones feats: everything earlier in the build, i.e. fixed species / background languages, the C8 origin card picks, earlier class language cards and fixed class languages at an earlier (class, level). A later class card picking Draconic is the duplicate (F3a-5), so the card does not appear. Known options are disabled "already known"; a stored pick of one is dropped with a `pruned` entry and the card goes pending.
- **Vanishing card.** When the earlier source is removed the card is removed from `choices` and a stored pick becomes a non-blocking `pruned` entry ("... no longer applies because you do not already know Draconic ..."). `_is_count_variable_choice` and `mark_unavailable` are not touched: the gate itself produces the `pruned` entries and the card always has 19 minus known options.
- **Note.** The FE10 "add it by hand" wording is removed from `feat_effects_tail` (Dragon's Tongue note now points at the card).
- **No new output keys** (`types.ts`, exports, frontend untouched).
- **Not done.** Greater/other feats with the same "if you already know" shape do not exist in the data (scan: only this feat).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, level 20): mixed Fighter 4.3, Wizard 4.2, Bard 11.2 (measured while two full suites ran); 2024 Fighter 2.8, Wizard 3.1, Bard 4.3 (limit 50).
- **Tests.** `tests/test_cult_of_the_dragon_language.py` (21, rule matrix in the docstring). Server suite 4029 -> 4050 passed (F0 golden green, not regenerated), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched.

## RP4 (A4, open question 16) 2024 Fighting Style feat card sees a Fighting Initiate style (2026-10-23)

Golden `test_c_series_golden.py` untouched and green (not regenerated). 2024 only (ruleset policy table: A4).

- **Rule** (catalog text): Fighting Initiate "If you already have a style, the one you choose must be different."; XPHB Fighter Fighting Style "gain a Fighting Style feat of your choice". Same style = same name key, so Dueling (TCE fighter style) equals the feat Dueling (XPHB).
- **Change.** `resolve._fi_seen_add(seen, catalog, feat, stored, ruleset)`: under ruleset 2024 a valid stored pick of the six fighter styles on the feat's `:fighting-style` card adds `seen["name:<style>"] = "<Style>, Fighting Initiate"` (setdefault, first holder wins). Called (1) in `_resolve_asi_selected_feats` right after the ASI feat is accepted (so its position is its (class index, level), after the cards of that level), and (2) for species-granted feats (origin position) into the initial `seen`. Later FS feat cards and later ASI picks of the FS feat read `seen` as before: option disabled "already taken (Dueling, Fighting Initiate)", a stored duplicate gives one `feat-duplicate-*` problem, nothing in `pruned` (the FS card is a holder in `_gate_duplicate_picks`, so no double report).
- **Unchanged.** FS first then FI (FE9: the FI card is disabled and pruned), mixed / 2014 (SRD style cards keep the FE9 `pruned` path, no problem).
- **Origin position.** Species feat grants can hold Fighting Initiate (Variant Human offers category-less feats). Background-granted feats do not include it, so backgrounds are not read. Lessons of the First Ones offers only Origin feats, so not Fighting Initiate. A Fighting Initiate whose prerequisite (martial weapon proficiency) fails is still recorded, like every feat name in the walk.
- **No new output keys.**
- **Tests.** `tests/test_fighting_initiate_fs_card_2024.py` (11; 4 fail without the change).
- **Timing** (`resolve()`, mean of 20, ms, level 20): mixed Fighter 3.1, Wizard 3.2, Bard 3.1; 2024 Fighter 2.0, Wizard 3.3, Bard 3.7 (limit 50).
- **Totals.** Server suite 4050 -> 4061 passed (F0 golden green, not regenerated), root suite 405 passed / 8 skipped; frontend untouched.
